Layout invariants:

  • All crates under crates/; workspace root Cargo.toml lists them all — no path drift.
  • conformance/ fixtures are canonical ground-truth; CI fails if moe-runtime diverges.
  • fuzz/ targets compile against release profile; UB detected via AddressSanitizer.
  • benchmarks/baselines/ committed; bench.yml fails PR if p50 latency regresses >5%.
  • docs/adr/ numbered sequentially; every tradeoff with rejected alternatives gets an ADR.
  • papers/ not published to crates.io; gitignored in release artifact builds.
  • tools/ are Python helpers, not part of the Rust workspace; separate pyproject.toml.

11. Core Schema & Types

11.1 Primitive Newtypes

ExpertId(u32)
  ownership: Copy; no heap
  mutation: immutable after construction
  concurrency: Send+Sync; safe to share across threads
  persistence: serialized as u32 le in wire/checkpoint formats
  compatibility: repr(transparent); stable ABI
  invariant: value < num_experts; violated => MoeError::InvalidExpertId

TokenIdx(u32)
  ownership: Copy; no heap
  mutation: immutable after construction
  concurrency: Send+Sync
  persistence: serialized as u32 le
  compatibility: repr(transparent); stable ABI
  invariant: value < n_tokens in current dispatch batch

CapacityFactor(f32)
  ownership: Copy
  invariant: finite, > 0.0; typical range [0.5, 2.0]
  failure: NaN/Inf/<=0 => MoeError::InvalidCapacityFactor at construction
  persistence: stored in MoeConfig JSON as "capacity_factor": float

11.2 DType and Device

DType enum
  variants: F32, BF16, F16, I8 (quantized expert weights)
  ownership: Copy
  concurrency: Send+Sync
  serialization: lowercase string in JSON config ("f32", "bf16", …)
  constraint: GateLogits always F32 regardless of model dtype
  constraint: GEMM backend must support requested dtype or return MoeError::UnsupportedDtype

Device enum
  variants: Cpu { num_threads: usize }, Cuda { ordinal: u32 }
  ownership: Clone (cheap)
  concurrency: Send+Sync; ordinal identifies CUDA device, not a stream
  failure: Cuda ordinal >= device count => MoeError::DeviceNotFound at init
  persistence: not serialized in checkpoints; runtime configuration only

11.3 Layout

Layout struct
  fields: offset: usize, strides: SmallVec<[usize; 4]>
  ownership: Clone; no heap for rank<=4 (SmallVec inline)
  mutation: immutable after tensor view creation; new Layout per slice/transpose
  concurrency: Send+Sync (no interior mutability)
  invariant: strides.len() == shape.len(); offset + sum(dim_i * stride_i) < storage_len
  failure: violated stride invariant => MoeError::LayoutViolation (detected in debug mode)
  serialization: not persisted; reconstructed from shape + contiguity assumption on load
  rejected alt: single stride-free contiguous-only design — needed for transpose/slice in dispatch

11.4 Tensor

Tensor struct
  fields:
    dtype: DType
    shape: SmallVec<[usize; 4]>   // rank-0..4 inline; heap for rank>4 (rare)
    device: Device
    layout: Layout
    storage: Arc<ArenaBlock>       // ref-counted block in TensorArena
  ownership: Arc-shared; cloning a Tensor is a view (zero-copy); exclusive mutation requires Arc::make_mut or unique handle
  mutation semantics:
    - Tensor is logically immutable (functional pipeline)
    - in-place ops require caller to hold only Arc reference count == 1 (checked at runtime in debug)
    - DispatchPlan scatter writes into pre-allocated output Tensor owned by dispatch stage
  concurrency:
    - multiple readers: safe (Arc)
    - concurrent writers: forbidden; enforced by &mut Tensor in all write APIs
    - cross-thread send: Tensor: Send+Sync because ArenaBlock: Send+Sync
  persistence: not directly serialized; weights loaded via ExpertWeights loader
  compatibility: shape/dtype/layout are public stable fields; ArenaBlock internals opaque
  failure modes:
    - shape mismatch in GEMM -> MoeError::ShapeMismatch { expected, got }
    - wrong device -> MoeError::DeviceMismatch
    - storage freed before last view dropped -> impossible (Arc prevents it)
  pitfall: aliased views to same storage with overlapping writes -> UB if both &mut; API prevents by requiring unique Arc

TensorArena struct
  purpose: bump-allocate storage blocks; reclaim whole arena per forward pass
  fields: blocks: Vec<Box<[u8]>>, bump_ptr: usize, device: Device
  ownership: owned by MoeLayer; passed by &mut into gate/dispatch/expert/combine stages
  mutation: &mut required; not Send (pinned to one thread/stream)
  lifecycle: reset() at forward-pass start reclaims all blocks O(1)
  failure: out-of-memory -> MoeError::ArenaOom { requested_bytes }
  invariant: all Tensors allocated from arena become invalid after reset(); callers must not retain Arc refs across reset boundary
  pitfall: forgetting reset() -> arena grows unboundedly; telemetry tracks arena high-water mark

11.5 GateLogits

GateLogits struct
  fields:
    logits: Tensor    // shape [n_tokens, num_experts], dtype F32
    n_tokens: usize
    num_experts: usize
  ownership: produced by moe-gate; consumed once by top-k selection; not cloned
  mutation: immutable after construction; gate module writes, router reads
  concurrency: single-threaded within gate stage; passed by value to router
  invariant: logits.shape == [n_tokens, num_experts]; dtype == F32
  serialization: not persisted; recomputed each forward pass
  failure: shape mismatch at construction -> MoeError::ShapeMismatch

11.6 RoutingDecision

RoutingDecision struct
  fields:
    decisions: Vec<[(ExpertId, f32); K]>   // K = top-k; per-token expert assignments + softmax weights
    n_tokens: usize
    k: usize
  ownership: produced by moe-gate router; consumed by moe-dispatch; single owner, passed by value
  mutation: immutable after construction
  concurrency: Send (passed across stage boundary)
  invariant:
    - decisions.len() == n_tokens
    - for each token: sum of K weights == 1.0 (softmax-over-topk normalized); assert in debug
    - ExpertId values < num_experts
    - weights >= 0.0 (post-softmax)
  tie-break: equal logits broken by ascending ExpertId (routing determinism invariant)
  serialization: not persisted; debug-serializable to JSON for conformance fixtures
  failure: invalid ExpertId -> MoeError::InvalidExpertId; weight sum != 1.0 -> MoeError::RoutingWeightError (debug only)

11.7 DispatchPlan

DispatchPlan struct
  fields:
    perm: Vec<u32>                 // permutation: perm[i] = original TokenIdx for dispatched slot i
    per_expert_counts: Vec<u32>    // len = num_experts; count of tokens routed to each expert (post-drop)
    dropped: BitSet                // bit i set iff TokenIdx(i) was dropped (capacity overflow)
    capacity: Capacity             // computed = ceil(capacity_factor * n_tokens * k / num_experts)
    n_tokens: usize
    num_experts: usize
  ownership: produced by moe-dispatch plan stage; consumed by scatter and a2a stages; single owner
  mutation: immutable after plan construction; scatter reads perm; gather uses per_expert_counts
  concurrency: Send; used across thread boundary between plan and scatter steps
  invariants:
    - perm.len() == sum(per_expert_counts) (total dispatched slots, not counting duplicates for k>1)
    - per_expert_counts[e] <= capacity for all e (capacity invariant)
    - dropped.len() == n_tokens
    - dropped tokens not present in perm (token conservation)
    - non-dropped tokens appear exactly k times in perm across all expert slots
  serialization: debug-serializable (JSON) for conformance fixtures; not persisted in checkpoints
  failure:
    - per_expert_counts[e] > capacity -> MoeError::CapacityOverflow (should never reach construction; caught in plan step)
    - perm contains TokenIdx >= n_tokens -> MoeError::InvalidTokenIdx
  pitfall: perm uses u32 indices; n_tokens up to ~64K safe; assert n_tokens <= u32::MAX at layer init

11.8 CombineBuffer

CombineBuffer struct
  fields:
    data: Tensor            // shape [n_tokens, d_model], dtype = model dtype; accumulator for combine step
    gate_weights: Tensor    // shape [n_tokens, k], dtype F32; normalized weights for weighted sum
    dropped_mask: BitSet    // mirrors DispatchPlan::dropped; dropped tokens get residual passthrough
  ownership: allocated in TensorArena per forward pass; consumed by combine step to produce output Tensor
  mutation: data written by combine gather; gate_weights read-only after dispatch plan
  concurrency: single-threaded combine (or partitioned by token range, non-overlapping writes)
  invariant:
    - data.shape[0] == n_tokens; data.shape[1] == d_model
    - gate_weights.shape == [n_tokens, k]
    - for each non-dropped token: sum over k of gate_weights[t, :] == 1.0 (softmax-normalized, inherited from RoutingDecision)
    - dropped token slots in data: written with residual input (passthrough); gate_weights unused for those tokens
  failure: d_model mismatch vs input tensor -> MoeError::ShapeMismatch
  pitfall: zero-initialize data before combine scatter to avoid accumulating garbage from prior forward pass

11.9 ExpertWeights

ExpertWeights struct
  fields:
    w_in:  Tensor    // shape [d_ff, d_model] for this rank's expert shard
    w_out: Tensor    // shape [d_model, d_ff] for this rank's expert shard
    expert_id: ExpertId
    ep_rank: u32     // which EP rank owns this expert shard
    dtype: DType
  ownership: loaded once at init; Arc-shared into expert FFN kernels; not cloned per forward pass
  mutation: immutable during forward pass; re-loaded only on weight hot-swap (future phase)
  concurrency: Send+Sync (Arc<Tensor> storage is read-only during forward)
  sharding invariant: expert e lives on EP rank (e.value % world_size); w_in/w_out contain only this rank's slice
  persistence: loaded from safetensors layout; key format "experts.{expert_id}.w_in" / "experts.{expert_id}.w_out"
  compatibility: shape [d_ff, d_model] is canonical regardless of EP sharding; sharding expressed via Layout offset/stride
  failure: missing key in checkpoint -> MoeError::WeightLoadError; shape mismatch -> MoeError::ShapeMismatch
  pitfall: d_ff may differ per expert in heterogeneous MoE; validate per-expert shape at load time

11.10 AuxLossState

AuxLossState struct
  fields:
    importance: Vec<f32>    // len = num_experts; sum of softmax weights routed to each expert (for importance loss)
    load:       Vec<f32>    // len = num_experts; fraction of tokens routed to each expert (for load-balance loss)
    z_logits:   Vec<f32>    // len = num_experts; log-sum-exp of gate logits per expert (for z-loss)
    n_tokens:   usize
    num_experts: usize
  ownership: produced by moe-gate alongside RoutingDecision; consumed by backward to compute aux gradients
  mutation: written once during gate forward; read during backward; not modified between
  concurrency: single-threaded within gate; passed by value to loss computation
  invariant:
    - importance.len() == load.len() == num_experts
    - sum(load) ~= k (each token routes to k experts; floating point tolerance 1e-4)
    - importance[e] >= 0 for all e
  serialization: not persisted; recomputed each forward pass; logged to telemetry per step
  failure: num_experts mismatch -> MoeError::DimensionMismatch

11.11 EpGroup

EpGroup struct
  fields:
    rank:       u32
    world_size: u32
    transport:  Arc<dyn Transport>
  ownership: Arc-shared across moe-comm and moe-sched; initialized once at runtime startup
  mutation: immutable after init; transport is a trait object with interior mutability for connection state
  concurrency: Send+Sync; multiple stages may hold Arc<EpGroup>
  invariant:
    - rank < world_size
    - expert e lives on rank (e.value % world_size); enforced at weight load and dispatch plan construction
    - all ranks in EpGroup must agree on world_size (checked during transport handshake)
  persistence: not serialized; reconstructed from env vars (MOE_EP_WORLD_SIZE, MOE_EP_RANK) at startup
  failure:
    - rank >= world_size -> MoeError::InvalidEpRank
    - transport init failure -> MoeError::CommInitError(String)
  pitfall: world_size=1 (single-rank) still requires EpGroup; all-to-all becomes a no-op memcpy in InProc transport

Transport trait (object-safe)
  methods:
    fn all_to_all(&self, send: &[u8], send_counts: &[usize], recv: &mut [u8], recv_counts: &[usize]) -> Result<(), MoeError>
    fn all_reduce_sum_f32(&self, buf: &mut [f32]) -> Result<(), MoeError>
    fn barrier(&self) -> Result<(), MoeError>
  variants:
    InProcTransport  -- uses crossbeam channels; world_size <= logical cores; for tests + single-node sim
    MultiProcTransport -- shims MPI_Alltoallv / NCCL all-to-all; activated when MOE_EP_WORLD_SIZE > 1 && feature="mpi"
  invariant: all_to_all byte counts must be symmetric across ranks (EP consistency invariant); validated in debug mode
  failure: bytes miscount -> MoeError::A2aCountMismatch { rank, expected, got }
  rejected alt: hard-coding MPI directly in moe-comm -> breaks single-node tests; Transport trait decouples

11.12 MoeConfig

MoeConfig struct
  fields:
    num_experts:     u32          // total experts across all EP ranks
    k:               u32          // top-k; 1 <= k <= num_experts
    capacity_factor: CapacityFactor
    ep_size:         u32          // EP world_size; must divide num_experts
    d_model:         usize
    d_ff:            usize
    dtype:           DType
    activation:      ActivationKind  // SwiGLU | ReLU | GeLU
    noisy_gate:      bool
    expert_choice:   bool
    aux_loss_coeff:  f32
    z_loss_coeff:    f32
    shared_expert:   bool
  ownership: Arc-shared; loaded once from JSON config file; immutable during training/inference run
  mutation: immutable after construction; modifying requires re-init of MoeLayer
  concurrency: Send+Sync
  serialization: serde JSON; all fields required except optional aux/z loss coefficients (default 0.0)
  invariants:
    - 1 <= k <= num_experts
    - ep_size divides num_experts
    - capacity_factor.0 > 0.0
    - expert_choice XOR noisy_gate (cannot both be true)
  failure: constraint violation at deserialization -> MoeError::ConfigError(String)

11.13 MoeLayer

MoeLayer struct
  fields:
    config:    Arc<MoeConfig>
    ep_group:  Arc<EpGroup>
    experts:   Vec<Arc<ExpertWeights>>   // only this rank's local experts (len = num_experts / ep_size)
    gate_proj: Tensor                     // W_g: shape [d_model, num_experts]; gate linear projection
    shared_expert: Option<Arc<ExpertWeights>>
    arena:     TensorArena               // per-layer arena; reset each forward call
    telemetry: Arc<TelemetrySink>
  ownership: owned by caller (model runtime or moe-cli); not cloned
  mutation: &mut Self required for forward() (arena reset is mutating); weight fields immutable
  concurrency: not Send (TensorArena pinned to caller thread); wrap in Mutex<MoeLayer> for multi-thread callers
  lifecycle: ::new(config, ep_group, weights, telemetry) -> Result<Self, MoeError>; drop releases arena + weights Arc
  public API:
    fn forward(&mut self, input: &Tensor, train: bool) -> Result<(Tensor, Option<AuxLossState>), MoeError>
  invariants:
    - input.shape == [n_tokens, config.d_model]
    - output.shape == input.shape (token conservation)
    - train=false: AuxLossState returned as None; aux/z loss not computed
  failure modes:
    - input shape violation -> MoeError::ShapeMismatch
    - arena OOM -> MoeError::ArenaOom
    - comm failure -> MoeError::CommError(Box<dyn Error>)
    - GEMM FFI error -> MoeError::GemmError(String)

11.14 MoeError

MoeError enum (non_exhaustive)
  variants:
    ShapeMismatch       { expected: SmallVec<[usize;4]>, got: SmallVec<[usize;4]> }
    DimensionMismatch   { context: &'static str, expected: usize, got: usize }
    InvalidExpertId     { id: u32, num_experts: u32 }
    InvalidTokenIdx     { idx: u32, n_tokens: u32 }
    InvalidCapacityFactor(f32)
    CapacityOverflow    { expert_id: ExpertId, count: usize, capacity: usize }
    RoutingWeightError  { token_idx: usize, weight_sum: f32 }
    DeviceMismatch      { expected: Device, got: Device }
    DeviceNotFound      { ordinal: u32 }
    UnsupportedDtype    { dtype: DType, context: &'static str }
    LayoutViolation     (String)
    ArenaOom            { requested_bytes: usize, available: usize }
    WeightLoadError     (String)
    ConfigError         (String)
    GemmError           (String)
    CommInitError       (String)
    CommError           (Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>)
    A2aCountMismatch    { rank: u32, expected: usize, got: usize }
    InvalidEpRank       { rank: u32, world_size: u32 }
  traits: std::error::Error, Display, Debug, Send, Sync
  ownership: heap-allocated message strings only in leaf variants; others are Copy-friendly
  conversion: From<std::io::Error> -> MoeError::WeightLoadError; From<serde_json::Error> -> MoeError::ConfigError
  serialization: not persisted; logged via tracing::error! with structured fields at error site
  invariant: no variant captures raw pointers or non-Send types
  pitfall: CommError boxes dyn Error to avoid pulling comm-crate types into moe-core; keep moe-core dep-free

11.15 TelemetrySink and TelemetryReport

TelemetrySink trait (object-safe)
  methods:
    fn record_expert_utilization(&self, step: u64, utils: &[f32])  // utils[e] in [0,1]
    fn record_drop_rate(&self, step: u64, rate: f32)
    fn record_routing_entropy(&self, step: u64, entropy: f32)
    fn record_stage_latency(&self, step: u64, stage: StageName, nanos: u64)
    fn flush(&self)
  implementations: NullSink (tests/benches), LogSink (tracing-based), MetricsSink (prometheus-compatible counters)
  concurrency: &self (shared ref); interior mutability behind Mutex or atomic counters
  ownership: Arc<dyn TelemetrySink> passed to MoeLayer at construction

StageName enum
  variants: Gate, Dispatch, A2aSend, ExpertGemm, A2aRecv, Combine
  ownership: Copy

TelemetryReport struct
  fields:
    step:              u64
    expert_utils:      Vec<f32>     // len = num_experts; utilization per expert
    drop_rate:         f32          // fraction of tokens dropped this step
    routing_entropy:   f32          // H = -sum p_e log p_e over expert routing distribution
    stage_latencies_ns: HashMap<StageName, u64>
    aux_loss:          Option<f32>
    z_loss:            Option<f32>
  serialization: serde JSON + CSV (for bench output); all fields public
  ownership: value type; produced by TelemetrySink::flush aggregation; not stored long-term in runtime

11.16 Stream and DoubleBuffer

Stream enum (moe-sched)
  variants:
    CpuThread { handle: Arc<ThreadPool> }
    CudaStream { ptr: *mut c_void, device: u32 }   // opaque CUDA stream handle; unsafe Send impl
  ownership: Arc-shared between overlap coordinator and individual stages
  concurrency:
    CpuThread: Send+Sync (ThreadPool is Arc-wrapped)
    CudaStream: Send via unsafe impl; single consumer per stream enforced by scheduler
  failure: CudaStream creation failure -> MoeError::DeviceNotFound

DoubleBufferState enum (moe-sched)
  variants: Idle, CommActive { buf_idx: u8 }, ComputeActive { buf_idx: u8 }, BothActive
  transitions:
    Idle -> CommActive(0) on a2a launch
    CommActive(i) -> BothActive on compute launch
    BothActive -> ComputeActive(i^1) on comm completion
    ComputeActive(i) -> Idle on compute completion
  ownership: owned by OverlapCoordinator; not Send (single scheduler thread)
  invariant: buf_idx in {0,1}; compute and comm never write same buffer index simultaneously
  failure: invalid state transition -> debug_assert! + MoeError::SchedulerStateError (in release: log + continue)

11.17 Capacity Computation

fn compute_capacity(capacity_factor: CapacityFactor, n_tokens: usize, k: u32, num_experts: u32) -> Capacity
  formula: ceil(capacity_factor.0 * (n_tokens * k as usize) as f32 / num_experts as f32)
  return type: Capacity(usize)
  invariants:
    - result >= 1 (minimum 1 token slot per expert regardless of factor)
    - if capacity_factor == inf (large float) -> capacity = n_tokens * k (no dropping; dense equivalent)
  failure: overflow usize on extreme inputs -> MoeError::CapacityOverflow guard before computation
  determinism: pure function; same inputs -> same output; no RNG

Capacity(usize) newtype
  ownership: Copy
  compatibility: repr(transparent); used in DispatchPlan and CombineBuffer size assertions

11.18 Type Compatibility and Versioning

Type Stable ABI Serialization Format Breaking Change Policy
ExpertId, TokenIdx yes (repr(transparent) u32) le u32 in binary; number in JSON never change repr
MoeConfig no (fields may grow) JSON with #[serde(default)] for new fields minor version bump; old configs remain valid
DispatchPlan no JSON debug only (conformance) internal; not persisted
ExpertWeights yes (shape-based) safetensors + JSON manifest key rename = major version
TelemetryReport no JSON + CSV additive fields ok; removal = minor bump
MoeError non_exhaustive not serialized new variants = minor; variant rename = major
Transport trait yes (object-safe) n/a method addition = major (breaks dyn)

Cross-type invariant summary:

  • DispatchPlan::capacity == compute_capacity(config.capacity_factor, n_tokens, config.k, config.num_experts) — checked at plan construction.
  • RoutingDecision::k == MoeConfig::k — enforced in gate output validation.
  • ExpertWeights count per rank == config.num_experts / config.ep_size — enforced at MoeLayer::new.
  • CombineBuffer::data.shape[0] == input.shape[0] (n_tokens) — enforced at combine stage entry.
  • All Tensor shapes validated against MoeConfig at stage boundaries in debug; sampled 1-in-100 in release.

12. Phase 0 — Skeleton — crates, Tensor handle, single dense FFN expert, top-1 route-one-token-and-combine smoke test (no comm, capacity=inf)

0.1 Objectives

  • Establish all 10 workspace crates (moe-core, moe-gate, moe-dispatch, moe-expert, moe-comm, moe-sched, moe-runtime, moe-telemetry, moe-cli, moe-bench) with Cargo.toml manifests, edition 2021, MSRV 1.78, [workspace] resolver = 2.
  • Define normative Tensor handle + arena allocator in moe-core; all other crates depend on it but do not define storage.
  • Implement one dense FFN expert (single GEMM-stub, no real BLAS FFI yet) in moe-expert.
  • Implement stub top-1 router (argmax, no softmax) in moe-gate.
  • Implement trivial dispatch (identity permutation, capacity=inf) + combine (copy-with-scalar-weight) in moe-dispatch.
  • Wire moe-runtime::MoeLayer::forward for the single-expert, single-token, single-device happy path.
  • Smoke test: route 1 token to expert 0, FFN computes, combine recovers output — bit-exact against Python reference.
  • Constraints introduced: DType enum (F32 only this phase), Device::Cpu only, Layout::RowMajor only, no async, no unsafe outside FFI stubs.

0.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P11 (ADR-05 arena), P6 (shape-first API), ADR-01, ADR-02, ADR-20 (thiserror), I1 (token conservation seed), I3 (routing determinism seed)

moe-core / src/lib.rs, tensor.rs, arena.rs, error.rs, types.rs

  • DType: F32 | F16 | BF16; Device: Cpu | Cuda(u32); Layout: RowMajor | ColMajor. (ADR-05 shape-first)
  • Arena: bump allocator wrapping Vec<u8> with 64-byte alignment; Arena::alloc(n_bytes) -> *mut u8; Arena::reset(). (P3 — bounded allocation; ADR-05)
  • Tensor { id: TensorId, dtype: DType, shape: SmallVec<[usize;4]>, device: Device, layout: Layout, byte_offset: usize, arena_ref: Arc<ArenaHandle> } — handle only; all arithmetic-free. (ADR-05)
  • TensorId(u64) — monotonic counter in Arc<AtomicU64>, unique per arena; plus ExpertId(u32), TokenIdx(u32), CapacityFactor(f32), Capacity(usize), K as const generic. (P11 shape-first types — §6.11)
  • GateLogits { data: Tensor, n_tokens: usize, num_experts: usize } — validates shape = [n_tokens, num_experts]. (P11)
  • RoutingDecision { decisions: Vec<[(ExpertId, f32); K]>, n_tokens: usize }. (I3 — type encodes routing determinism contract)
  • DispatchPlan { perm: Vec<u32>, per_expert_counts: Vec<u32>, dropped: BitSet, capacity: Capacity }. (I1 — token conservation; I2 — capacity invariant)
  • CombineBuffer { data: Tensor }; AuxLossState { importance: f32, load: f32 }. (I8 — aux loss monotonicity)
  • MoeError: ShapeMismatch, CapacityOverflow, ExpertNotFound, CommError(String), GemmError(String), InvalidConfig(String). (ADR-20 thiserror; P6 fail fast)
  • BitSet: newtype over Vec<u64> with set/get/count — no external dep. Re-exports in lib.rs; #[deny(missing_docs)]. (ADR-23)

moe-gate / src/lib.rs, topk.rs

  • Router trait: fn route(&self, logits: &GateLogits) -> Result<RoutingDecision, MoeError>. (P11 shape-first; ADR-20)
  • TopKRouter { k: usize, num_experts: usize } — Phase 0: argmax only (k=1), no softmax, returns weight=1.0. (P3 Switch Transformer k=1 baseline)
  • topk_indices(row: &[f32], k: usize) -> SmallVec<[usize; 8]> — partial-sort; tie-break by index ascending. (I3 routing determinism — §7)
  • Observability hook: tracing::trace! span gate.route with n_tokens, k. (P4 observability by default; ADR-11)

moe-expert / src/lib.rs, ffn.rs, gemm_stub.rs

  • ExpertWeights { w_in: Tensor, w_out: Tensor, bias_in: Option<Tensor>, bias_out: Option<Tensor> }. (I7 weight shard completeness)
  • Expert trait: fn forward(&self, input: Tensor) -> Result<Tensor, MoeError>.
  • DenseExpert::forward: GEMM(w_in) -> activation -> GEMM(w_out) — all on Arena scratch; no heap alloc during forward. (P3 bounded resources; ADR-05)
  • gemm_stub::matmul_f32 — naive triple loop; replaced by BLAS FFI in Phase 4. (P10 correctness before optimization — §6.10)

moe-dispatch / src/lib.rs, plan.rs, scatter.rs, combine.rs

  • Dispatcher trait: fn plan(&self, rd: &RoutingDecision, capacity: Capacity) -> Result<DispatchPlan, MoeError>. (I2 capacity invariant)
  • combine: un-permute via inverse of perm, gate-weighted sum; asserts sum(weights) ≈ 1.0 ± 1e-6. (I5 combine correctness — §7)
  • Drop tracking: dropped: BitSet — Phase 0 always empty; residual passthrough path coded but not triggered. (I1 token conservation)

moe-runtime / src/lib.rs, layer.rs

  • MoeLayer::forward(input: Tensor) -> Result<Tensor, MoeError>: gate -> dispatch -> per-expert forward -> combine. (I1 token conservation end-to-end)
  • Phase 0: single expert, k=1, synchronous, no EP, telemetry stubs only. (P10 correctness first)

Remaining Phase-0 crates (stub level)

  • moe-telemetry/src/lib.rs — stub Telemetry::noop(); all record calls no-op. (ADR-11/12; P4 — zero-overhead when disabled)
  • moe-cli/src/main.rs — clap-based; subcommand run loads JSON config, calls forward. (ADR-10 TOML config; ADR-20 anyhow)
  • moe-bench/benches/smoke.rs — criterion bench: 1-token forward, baseline wall-clock. (ADR-16)
  • Tests: moe-core/tests/tensor_roundtrip.rs, moe-gate/tests/topk_argmax.rs, moe-expert/tests/ffn_identity.rs, moe-dispatch/tests/identity_dispatch.rs, moe-runtime/tests/smoke_e2e.rs. (ADR-14)

0.3 Deliverable

  • All 10 crates compile (cargo build --workspace) with zero warnings under #[deny(warnings)].
  • cargo test --workspace passes all unit + integration tests.
  • moe-runtime/tests/smoke_e2e.rs: 1 token, 1 expert, k=1, d_model=4, d_ffn=8 — output matches Python reference (numpy matmul + relu + matmul) to 1e-5 relative error.
  • moe-cli run --config examples/smoke.json exits 0, prints output shape: [1, 4].
  • Criterion smoke bench runs without panic; wall-clock baseline recorded to target/criterion/.
  • cargo clippy --workspace -- -D clippy::all clean.

0.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
cargo test --workspace 100% pass, 0 ignored
Smoke E2E relative error ≤ 1e-5 vs numpy reference
Compile time (cold, debug) ≤ 60 s on CI (4-core)
Binary size moe-cli (release) ≤ 5 MB

0.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/phase0/smoke_input.json [1,4] f32 token, weights W_in[4,8], W_out[8,4] output tensor [1,4] values listed
fixtures/phase0/smoke_reference.npy numpy-generated ground truth bit-pattern for f32 comparison
fixtures/phase0/config_smoke.json LayerConfig for 1-expert smoke parsed without error
fixtures/phase0/malformed_shape.json Tensor shape [0,4] MoeError::ShapeMismatch
fixtures/phase0/malformed_dtype.json unknown dtype string MoeError::InvalidConfig
fixtures/phase0/replay.bin serialized Arena state (Phase 0 only for regression) deterministic byte-identical replay

0.6 Pitfalls

  • Arena alignment: Arena::alloc must guarantee 64-byte alignment; naive bump misaligns SIMD loads. Signal: SIGBUS on AVX load in Phase 4. Debug: assert_eq!(ptr as usize % 64, 0) in alloc.
  • TensorId collision: two arenas using separate counters produce non-unique IDs if Arc<AtomicU64> is not shared at workspace level. Signal: combine mistakenly aliases input/output. Debug: assert distinct IDs before combine write.
  • Dtype widening silently: gemm_stub reads Tensor.dtype but Phase 0 only handles F32; passing F16 tensor silently reinterprets bytes. Signal: NaN outputs. Fix: assert_eq!(a.dtype, DType::F32) guard at top of stub.
  • Combine weight sum drift: gate weight=1.0 in top-1 trivial case passes, but floating-point sum assertion fires when weights computed from real softmax (Phase 1). Loosen tolerance to 1e-4 now to avoid false failures later.
  • SmallVec shape overflow: shapes with > 4 dims spill to heap, unexpected alloc in arena-only forward (signal: allocator counter mismatch; fix: cap SmallVec<[usize;4]>, reject 5-D at GateLogits ctor). MSRV 1.78 breakage: accidental std::simd (1.89+) or LazyLock (1.80+) fails CI; fix: rust-toolchain.toml pins 1.78.0, CI runs cargo +1.78.0 check.

0.7 Performance Concerns

  • Arena reset frequency: Arena::reset() at layer boundary; leaking across layers causes unbounded growth. Measure peak arena bytes via Arena::used() counter.
  • gemm_stub triple loop: O(mnk) naive; acceptable for Phase 0 (d_model=4, d_ffn=8 in smoke), catastrophic at real dims. Profile with cargo bench to establish baseline before BLAS swap.
  • Vec<Box<dyn Expert>>: vtable dispatch per expert token batch, acceptable for Phase 0 (revisit at large expert count). Criterion overhead: bench harness allocates — ensure black_box wraps output to prevent dead-code elimination.

0.8 Edge Cases

  • n_tokens=0: GateLogits shape [0, num_experts] — Dispatcher::plan returns empty DispatchPlan, combine returns zero-row tensor. Test: moe-runtime/tests/empty_batch.rs.
  • d_model=1: valid, GEMM degenerates to dot product (test included). num_experts=1, k=1: trivial routing, all tokens to expert 0, permutation=identity, combine weight=1.0.
  • Weight tensor uninitialized (all zeros): output is zero; combine output is zero; not a bug — test documents this explicitly.
  • Arena::reset() called mid-forward: undefined behavior; guard with RefCell<bool> in_forward flag; panic on violation.

0.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Arena OOM (alloc returns null) Arena::alloc returns Err(MoeError::InternalAlloc) entire forward aborts caller retries with smaller batch
gemm_stub NaN output output tensor is_nan() check post-GEMM combine propagates NaN to output log MoeError::GemmError, return Err
Shape mismatch W_in * input assert in gemm_stub or explicit check forward aborts operator fixes config JSON
TensorId aliasing debug assert in combine silent wrong output enable debug_assertions in CI
Clippy regression CI gate fails blocks merge fix lint; no disable allowed

0.10 Operational Notes

  • RUST_LOG=moe=trace enables tracing spans; Phase 0 stubs emit gate/dispatch/expert/combine span names for future use.
  • Arena::used() and Arena::capacity() exposed as u64 metrics; moe-telemetry::Telemetry::noop() drops them — future phases wire to prometheus.
  • moe-cli reads MOE_LOG_JSON=1 env var to switch to JSON log output (via tracing-subscriber::fmt::json()).
  • Rollback: crates are independent; reverting moe-expert to stub does not break moe-gate or moe-dispatch — by design.
  • Compatibility: LayerConfig JSON schema versioned ("version": 1); unknown fields rejected with MoeError::InvalidConfig to catch config drift early.

13. Phase 1 — Gating network — dense top-k router, softmax-over-topk, deterministic tie-break, router logits

1.1 Objectives

  • Replace stub argmax with full gating: linear projection W_g (d_model→num_experts), top-k selection, softmax-over-topk normalization, deterministic tie-break.
  • Implement noisy_topk: add N(0,1)·softplus(W_noise·x) noise per expert before selection (Shazeer 2017).
  • Compute GateLogits = x·W_g^T; return RoutingDecision with gate weights summing to 1 over top-k per token.
  • Enforce routing determinism invariant: identical input+seed → identical RoutingDecision/DispatchPlan bit-for-bit.
  • Compute AuxLossState{importance, load} (GShard/Switch aux loss) and router z-loss mean(log²(Σexp(logits))) (ST-MoE, Zoph 2022).
  • Expose logits + routing decisions via telemetry spans; compute routing entropy per token.
  • Constraints: CPU-only, no EP, capacity=inf (no dropping yet), k≥1, num_experts≥k.

1.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P1 (Shazeer 2017 noisy top-k, aux loss), P5 (Zoph 2022 z-loss), P3 (Switch k=1 baseline), I3 (routing determinism), I8 (aux loss monotonicity), I9 (gate logit safety), ADR-11 (tracing spans), ADR-20 (MoeError)

moe-gate/src/topk.rs — topk_indices_deterministic(row, k) -> SmallVec<[usize;8]>: partial sort descending via select_nth_unstable_by (no alloc for k≤8); tie-break: equal logits → lower ExpertId wins, via explicit (a_val,a_idx).cmp((b_val,b_idx)).reverse() comparator for cross-platform stability. (I3)

moe-gate/src/softmax.rs — softmax_over_topk(logits, indices) -> SmallVec<[f32;8]>: numerically stable (subtract row-max before exp); panics on NaN weight; invariant |Σw - 1.0| < 1e-5 else MoeError::InvalidConfig.

moe-gate/src/router.rs (rewrite) — RouterConfig{k, num_experts, noisy_topk, noise_seed, aux_loss_coeff, z_loss_coeff}; DenseRouter{w_g, w_noise, cfg, rng: Option<Xorshift64>}. Xorshift64 deterministic PRNG reseeded per forward from (layer_idx, batch_idx) for reproducibility. compute_logits: gemm_stub::matmul_f32([n,d_model]×[d_model,E]→[n,E]). Noisy top-k adds noise_i = N(0,1)·softplus(w_noise_i·x) (softplus=ln(1+exp(x))) before selection. route(): per token top-k → softmax → RoutingDecision. (P1)

moe-gate/src/aux_loss.rs — compute_aux_loss(rd, logits, cfg) -> AuxLossState: importance_e = Σ_tokens softmax_all_e(logits[t,e]) (full softmax over all experts); load_e = count(t: e∈top-k(t))/n_tokens; aux_loss = Σ_e(importance_e·load_e)·aux_loss_coeff; z_loss = mean_t(log²(Σ_e exp(logits[t,e])))·z_loss_coeff. Not backpropagated in Phase 1; stored for telemetry only. (P1, P5)

moe-gate/src/entropy.rs — routing_entropy(rd) -> f32: per-token H=-Σ_k w_k·log(w_k), averaged over tokens.

moe-gate/src/lib.rs — Router trait gains fn aux_loss(&self) -> AuxLossState (last computed, cached) alongside route().

moe-telemetry (Phase 1) — record_routing_entropy/record_aux_loss/record_z_loss (lock-free AtomicU32 bitcast); record_gate_logits_stats(min,max,mean) debug-level sampled 1-in-N; all no-op under Telemetry::noop(). (ADR-11)

moe-gate/tests/ — topk_correctness.rs (known logits → expected indices+weights, all-equal→tie-break by id), softmax_normalization.rs (Σw=1.0±1e-6, all w>0), determinism.rs (100 invocations identical), aux_loss.rs (uniform routing → importance·load = 1/E²·n_tokens per expert), z_loss.rs (vs numpy, 4-expert 4-token).

moe-runtime — wire DenseRouter into MoeLayer; LayerConfig gains RouterConfig.

1.3 Deliverable

  • DenseRouter::route operational for arbitrary n_tokens/num_experts/k, CPU only; weights sum to 1.0 per token (test-verified).
  • AuxLossState (importance, load, aux_loss, z_loss) computed and emitted via telemetry; routing entropy readable via Telemetry::snapshot().
  • Noisy top-k reproducible: same seed → same routing for any input shape. moe-cli run prints entropy+aux_loss to stderr under RUST_LOG=moe_gate=debug.

1.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
Σ weights_k per token |Σ - 1.0| ≤ 1e-5
Tie-break determinism 100/100 identical, same seed
Aux loss vs numpy reference ≤ 1e-5 relative error
Z-loss vs numpy reference ≤ 1e-5 relative error
Route latency (n=512, E=8, k=2, CPU) ≤ 2 ms p50 (criterion)
Memory per forward (n=512) 0 heap allocs outside Arena
Noisy top-k reproducibility deterministic over 1000 seeds

1.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/phase1/router_4e_4t_k2.json 4 experts, 4 tokens, k=2, explicit logits top-k indices + softmax weights
fixtures/phase1/router_tie_break.json Two experts, identical logits lower ExpertId wins consistently
fixtures/phase1/aux_loss_uniform.json Uniform logits, equal load aux_loss = aux_coeff · 1/E
fixtures/phase1/z_loss_reference.npy numpy z_loss, 4×8 logit matrix value ± 1e-5
fixtures/phase1/noisy_topk_seed42.json Seed=42, 8-token batch deterministic noise + routing
fixtures/phase1/malformed_k_gt_e.json k > num_experts MoeError::InvalidConfig
fixtures/phase1/malformed_nan_logits.json NaN in logit matrix MoeError::GemmError / NaN guard

1.6 Pitfalls

  • Softmax overflow: forgetting row-max subtraction before exp overflows to inf (signal: NaN weights; fix: always max-subtract, verify with large-logit fixture). Tie-break platform drift: bare f32::partial_cmp orders differently ARM vs x86 (fix: explicit (neg_val, idx) tuple sort).
  • Noise PRNG state leak: Xorshift64 state persisting across batches breaks reproducibility (signal: determinism test fails after batch 2; fix: reseed from (layer_idx XOR batch_token) each route() call).
  • Full softmax for importance: softmax_all(logits) for importance loss adds O(n·E) work, easy to forget and cause 2× slowdown — fuse with logit-computation loop.
  • Z-loss overflow: naive log(Σexp(logits)) overflows past logit 80 — use logsumexp = max + log(Σexp(logits-max)).
  • Weight tensor aliasing: w_g shared across threads when DenseRouter is Arc-wrapped — matmul must take &self, never &mut self.
  • k=0: RouterConfig must reject at construction; otherwise zero-length per-token arrays make combine silently output zeros.

1.7 Performance Concerns

  • Logit GEMM dominates: [n,d_model]×[d_model,E] — e.g. d_model=4096, E=64 → real BLAS cuts Phase-1 gemm_stub cost ~50×; establish baseline now.
  • Per-row topk allocation: SmallVec<[usize;8]> spills to heap for k>8 — hard-cap k≤8 in RouterConfig validation.
  • Importance full softmax: extra [n,E] exp pass; fuse with logit buffer already resident in cache.
  • Routing entropy: n_tokens floating-point log ops, negligible vs GEMM at Phase-1 scale.
  • Cache locality: [n_tokens,E] logits row-major (good top-k locality); per-token noise vector [E] fits L1.

1.8 Edge Cases

  • n_tokens=0: empty RoutingDecision; aux_loss=0, z_loss=0, entropy=0. k=num_experts: top-k=all experts, entropy maximized, weight-sum still validated.
  • All logits identical (e.g. zero-init weights): tie-break selects experts 0..k-1 always; aux_loss non-zero (load imbalance on tied experts).
  • Very large logit (1e38): softmax overflow guard exercised by max-f32 fixture. w_g all-zero: logits all-zero, tie-break fires every token (valid).
  • Noise magnitude explosion (large softplus input): cap noise at 10·sigma via RouterConfig::noise_clip to prevent routing collapse.

1.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
NaN in RoutingDecision weights assert!(w.is_finite()) post-softmax all downstream expert fwds get NaN MoeError::GemmError; abort forward
k > E misconfiguration RouterConfig::new validation construction-time panic operator fixes config
Determinism break (PRNG leak) determinism unit test non-reproducible outputs across restarts reseed guard at top of route()
Aux loss NaN (0·log(0)) checked in compute_aux_loss loss silently ignored treat load_e=0 as aux_e=0, log warning
Logit GEMM wrong shape pre-call shape assert MoeError returned operator fixes d_model/num_experts

1.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics per forward: routing_entropy, aux_loss, z_loss, gate_logits_{min,max,mean} (sampled). RUST_LOG=moe_gate=trace logs per-token top-k IDs+weights; =debug logs per-batch entropy+aux_loss (production-safe).
  • Tracing span moe.gate.route wraps DenseRouter::route; children: moe.gate.gemm, .topk, .softmax, .aux_loss. Telemetry::snapshot() -> TelemetrySnapshot is serde_json-serializable for CLI dump.
  • Config migration: RouterConfig v1→v2 adds z_loss_coeff default 0.0; unknown JSON fields → InvalidConfig.
  • Rollback: DenseRouter constructed per MoeLayer; reverting to Phase-0 TopKRouter is a config change, no binary change.

14. Phase 2 — Token dispatch + combine (single device) — permutation/scatter-gather, per-expert contiguous buffers, weighted combine (un-permute + gate-weighted sum)

2.1 Objectives

  • Implement full DispatchPlan construction: token→expert assignment from RoutingDecision, permutation array, capacity enforcement, deterministic lowest-weight drop, dropped: BitSet.
  • Build per-expert contiguous token buffers via scatter ([Capacity, d_model] slice per expert); Capacity = ceil(capacity_factor·n_tokens·k/num_experts).
  • Implement combine: un-permute expert outputs, gate-weighted sum, residual passthrough for dropped tokens (token conservation invariant): every output row is Σ_k(w_k·ffn_k(x)) or x.
  • Single-device (CPU), no EP, no all-to-all; moe-comm not invoked. Constraints: k≥1, capacity_factor>0, dropped tokens use input-tensor residual (no zero-fill).

2.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P2 (GShard capacity factor + drop policy), P3 (Switch capacity baseline, bounded resources), I1 (token conservation), I2 (capacity invariant), I5 (combine correctness), ADR-20 (MoeError::CapacityOverflow)

moe-dispatch/src/plan.rs (full rewrite) — CapacityDispatcher{capacity_factor, num_experts, k} implements Dispatcher::plan(rd, n_tokens, input) -> Result<DispatchPlan, MoeError>: compute capacity via integer ceiling (num+den-1)/den; walk tokens 0..n_tokens, for each (expert_e, weight_w) in rd.decisions[t] assign next free slot if per_expert_slots[e] < capacity, else mark overflow; resolve overflow per expert by weight-ascending drop until slots ≤ capacity, recording dropped: BitSet; build perm[e][s] = token_idx and final per_expert_counts. Asserts Σper_expert_counts ≤ n_tokens·k. DispatchPlan gains assignments: Vec<ExpertAssignment{token_idx, expert_id, slot, weight}> (scatter/combine helper, not on serialization hot path). Determinism: drop-sort key (weight_asc, token_idx_asc, expert_id_asc). (I2, P2)

moe-dispatch/src/scatter.rs — scatter_tokens(input, plan, d_model) -> Vec<Tensor>: one Arena tensor per expert shaped [per_expert_counts[e], d_model]; for each assignment, contiguous-row memcpy input[t,:]→expert_buf[e][s,:]; empty tensor for 0-count experts. Cache-friendly when assignments pre-sorted (expert_id, slot) in plan().

moe-dispatch/src/combine.rs (full rewrite) — combine_outputs(expert_outputs, plan, input, d_model) -> Result<Tensor, MoeError>: zero-init [n_tokens, d_model] from Arena; FMA accumulate output[t,:] += w·expert_outputs[e][s,:] per assignment; residual pass output[t,:] = input[t,:] for t∈dropped; post-condition Σassigned_weights[t] ≈ 1.0±1e-4 for non-dropped t. Combiner trait exposes combine(...). (I5, I1)

moe-dispatch/src/lib.rs — re-exports CapacityDispatcher, scatter_tokens, combine_outputs, DispatchPlan, ExpertAssignment; public Dispatcher+Combiner traits behind trait objects.

moe-runtime/src/layer.rs — forward flow: gate.route→RoutingDecision → dispatcher.plan→DispatchPlan → scatter_tokens→per_expert_inputs → per nonempty expert forward→expert_outputs[e] → combiner.combine→output → record_drop_rate(dropped.count()/(n_tokens·k)).

moe-telemetry — record_drop_rate(f32), record_capacity(usize), record_per_expert_loads(&[u32]) histogram.

Tests — scatter_gather.rs (8t/4e/k2/cap1.0, no drops, roundtrip=identity), capacity_drop.rs (cap=0.5, ~50% overflow dropped), drop_determinism.rs (100 runs identical bitset), token_conservation.rs (|output-Σw·ffn|<1e-5), residual_passthrough.rs (dropped token bit-exact to input), e2e_4expert_k2.rs (16t/4e/k2/cap1.25 vs numpy).

2.3 Deliverable

  • CapacityDispatcher::plan builds valid DispatchPlan for any (n_tokens, E, k, capacity_factor); scatter_tokens/combine_outputs satisfy token conservation.
  • Drop policy deterministic and reproducible from the same RoutingDecision; MoeLayer::forward operational for ≥4 experts, ≥64 tokens, k=2 on CPU.
  • Telemetry (drop_rate, capacity, per_expert_loads) emitted per forward; moe-cli run --config examples/4expert_k2.json exits 0, prints drop_rate to stderr.

2.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
Token conservation per non-dropped token |output - reference| ≤ 1e-5
Residual passthrough (dropped tokens) bit-exact: output[t] == input[t]
Drop determinism 100/100 identical dropped bitsets
Capacity invariant per_expert_counts[e] ≤ Capacity for all e
Σ assigned_weights[t] non-dropped |Σ - 1.0| ≤ 1e-4 per token
Scatter+combine latency (n=512, E=8, k=2) ≤ 5 ms CPU p50 (criterion)
Heap allocs per forward 0 outside Arena
cargo test moe-dispatch 100% pass

2.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/phase2/dispatch_8t_4e_k2_cap1.json 8 tokens, 4 experts, k=2, cap=1.0 DispatchPlan with per_expert_counts, 0 drops
fixtures/phase2/dispatch_drop_ref.json capacity_factor=0.25, high-drop scenario dropped bitset + drop count
fixtures/phase2/scatter_roundtrip.npy input→scatter→identity-ffn→combine output == input (uniform weights, k=E)
fixtures/phase2/combine_weighted.npy weighted combine reference from numpy ≤ 1e-5 error
fixtures/phase2/residual_passthrough.json all tokens dropped (capacity=0) output == input, exact
fixtures/phase2/malformed_rd_wrong_k.json RoutingDecision k mismatch vs config MoeError::InvalidConfig
fixtures/phase2/malformed_negative_cap.json capacity_factor ≤ 0 MoeError::InvalidConfig at construction
fixtures/phase2/replay_dispatch.bin serialized plan for regression byte-identical on replay

2.6 Pitfalls

  • Combine += without zero-init: unzeroed output[t,:] accumulates onto garbage (signal: non-deterministic large error; fix: Arena zero-on-alloc + memset guard). Dropped-token overwrite: combine processing dropped tokens with weight 0 clobbers residual (fix: skip dropped in assignment loop, apply residual in separate final pass).
  • Sort instability in drop selection: unstable sort changes which tokens drop on identical input (signal: bitset differs across runs; fix: stable sort on full (weight, token_idx, expert_idx) key).
  • Off-by-one in Capacity: floor instead of ceil underestimates capacity by 1, inflating drop_rate at boundary (fix: integer ceiling (num+den-1)/den).
  • Expert buffer shape mismatch: if expert forward reorders rows internally, slot→row mapping breaks combine — invariant: expert FFN must preserve row order (assert shape + integration test).
  • Empty expert forward: per_expert_counts[e]==0 must still yield a valid empty [0,d_model] tensor, not a null handle (signal: combine crashes on lookup; fix: pre-fill zero-row tensors).
  • Weight normalization post-drop: token assigned {0.3,0.4,0.3} with one expert over-capacity combines to 0.7, not 1.0 — spec deliberately does NOT re-normalize; fractional output for partially-dropped tokens is accepted and documented in ADR.

2.7 Performance Concerns

  • Assignment buffer size: n_tokens·k entries × ~24B = 96 KB at n=512,k=2 (fits L2); sort once by (expert_id, slot) in plan(), reuse for scatter.
  • Scatter memcpy pattern: source rows non-contiguous vs contiguous per-expert destination; for d_model=4096 (16 KB/row) prefetcher misses badly — mitigate via blocked scatter grouped by expert; future: SIMD gather.
  • Combine accumulation: FMA loop is k passes over d_model per token; order assignments (token_idx, expert) for output-cache reuse.
  • Zero-init overhead: memset of [n_tokens,d_model] (8 MB at n=512,d=4096) — amortize by resetting (not reallocating) the Arena region across batches.
  • BitSet ops: set/get O(1), count O(n_tokens/64); avoid iterating the set per combine step, use direct dropped.get(t).

2.8 Edge Cases

  • capacity_factor=inf: Capacity≈usize::MAX, no drops ever (tested against adversarial skewed routing). capacity_factor=0.0: rejected at construction (InvalidConfig); would otherwise drop all tokens, residual=input (valid but wasteful).
  • Extreme imbalance (all tokens→expert 0): per_expert_counts[0]=min(n_tokens·k,Capacity), dropped=n_tokens·k−Capacity, residual covers the rest (tested).
  • k=num_experts (dense routing): every token to every expert, capacity must accommodate all; combine output validated against dense-FFN-sum reference.
  • Single token k=1 (Phase-0 regression, no drop unless capacity=0); token with all k assignments dropped → output[t]=input[t], no NaN even under total overflow; d_model=1 legal (scalar-multiply GEMM), exercises full scatter/combine path.

2.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Combine += garbage (no zero-init) output vs reference > 1e-3, non-deterministic all tokens wrong output Arena zeroing guarantee; debug_assert in combine
Dropped token gets ffn output residual test: output[t] != input[t] dropped tokens have wrong values fix combine loop order; residual pass last
Capacity exceeded capacity invariant assert silent wrong routing assert in plan(); MoeError::CapacityOverflow
ExpertAssignment sort instability determinism test fails non-reproducible drops switch to stable_sort in plan()
Expert forward wrong shape shape assert in combine combine panics / indexes OOB MoeError::ShapeMismatch; forward aborts
Arena exhausted mid-scatter Arena::alloc returns Err forward aborts return MoeError; operator raises arena_bytes config
Empty expert_outputs[e] accessed combine reads slot 0 of empty tensor OOB panic pre-init all expert_outputs to zero-row tensor

2.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics per forward: dispatch.drop_rate, dispatch.capacity, dispatch.per_expert_loads (histogram), dispatch.assignments_total. RUST_LOG=moe_dispatch=debug logs per-expert counts+drop count; =trace logs each dropped token and its over-capacity expert.
  • Tracing spans: moe.dispatch.plan, .scatter, .combine. TelemetrySnapshot carries drop_rate + per_expert_loads; operator alert if drop_rate > 0.05 (configurable).
  • Config: CapacityDispatcher{capacity_factor, drop_policy}, drop_policy="lowest_weight" (Phase 2) or future "fifo", stored in LayerConfig::dispatch_cfg; unknown values → InvalidConfig. Rollback: dispatch_policy="identity" re-selects Phase-0 behavior (no drops, capacity=inf) for debugging.
  • Forward compatibility: DispatchPlan serialization (Phase 5 distributed EP) uses bincode, append-only field additions.

15. Phase 3 — Capacity and drop policy — capacity factor, per-expert capacity, deterministic token dropping, overflow handling, dropped-token residual passthrough

3.1 Objectives

Objective Constraint
Enforce Capacity = ceil(capacity_factor·n_tokens·K/num_experts) per expert Invariant: never allocate > Capacity slots in any per-expert buffer
Deterministic drop: identical input+seed → identical DispatchPlan.dropped BitSet Tie-break ascending ExpertId, then ascending TokenIdx
Residual passthrough: dropped tokens copy input embedding unchanged into output Zero grad flow through expert for dropped token; token conservation holds
Expose CapacityFactor as runtime-tunable (no recompile) Must propagate to moe-dispatch without restart
Drop-rate telemetry, per-expert and global, per forward moe-telemetry counter, not inline logging

Scope: moe-dispatch (capacity buffers+drop), moe-core (DispatchPlan.dropped BitSet), moe-telemetry (drop_rate counter); no changes to moe-gate/moe-expert. Constraints: capacity_factor ∈ (0.0, 10.0] (panic on 0/NaN at init); K·capacity_factor/num_experts must not overflow usize (checked at config parse); drop policy is stateless per forward call.

3.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P2 (GShard drop policy — lowest-gate-weight first), I1 (token conservation — residual passthrough), I2 (capacity invariant), I5 (combine correctness — weights sum to 1 post-drop), ADR-14 (differential test), ADR-15 (fuzz target)

moe-core/dispatch_plan.rs — DispatchPlan gains dropped: BitSet (fixedbitset, indexed by TokenIdx, I1), capacity: Capacity (computed ceil(...), stored for debugging), per_expert_counts: Vec<u32> (length exactly num_experts).

moe-dispatch/capacity.rs (new)

fn compute_capacity(cfg: &MoeConfig) -> Capacity
fn build_dispatch_plan(gate_out: &[RoutingDecision], cfg: &MoeConfig) -> DispatchPlan

build_dispatch_plan: iterate tokens in TokenIdx order (deterministic); per token, walk its topk pairs descending by gate weight; if per_expert_counts[e] < capacity assign+increment else mark dropped-candidate; a token drops iff ALL K experts reject it (Phase 3 does not support partial drop — avoids fractional combine weights, I5); build perm via stable sort of (ExpertId, slot_index) for contiguous per-expert layout. (P2, I2)

moe-dispatch/combine.rs — fn combine_with_residual(plan, expert_out, input) -> Tensor: non-dropped → Σ_k weight_k·expert_out_k[token]; dropped → copy input row verbatim, gate weights not applied. (I1)

moe-telemetry/counters.rs — TOKEN_DROP_TOTAL: AtomicU64 (global), per_expert_drops: Vec<AtomicU64> (len=num_experts), updated inside build_dispatch_plan post-BitSet (no mutex — one thread owns dispatch per forward).

moe-dispatch/lib.rs — CapacityFactor validated at MoeLayer::new, stored in Arc<MoeConfig> (RwLock-free post-init); layer.set_capacity_factor(f) takes &mut self (exclusive, no mid-forward mutation).

Tests — test_capacity_exact (8t/4e/K2/cf1.0→capacity=4, no drops under uniform load), test_capacity_overflow (overload expert 0 → dropped non-empty, all counts ≤ capacity), test_determinism (two identical calls → byte-identical BitSets), test_residual_passthrough (dropped row == input row), test_partial_load (K=2, 1 expert full → token still served by the other, non-dropped).

3.3 Deliverable

  • moe-dispatch compiles with capacity+drop logic; moe-core::DispatchPlan includes dropped BitSet; cargo test -p moe-dispatch all pass.
  • moe-telemetry exposes drop_rate_pct gauge readable via moe-cli --dump-stats; operators can inspect per-expert drop counts, global drop rate, and capacity value used per forward.
  • MoeLayer::forward accepts capacity_factor in LayerConfig; f32::INFINITY disables drops (capacity = n_tokens·K, no overflow possible).

3.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold / Condition
test_capacity_overflow passes Per-expert count ≤ capacity for all experts
test_determinism passes Bit-identical BitSet across 100 repeated calls
test_residual_passthrough passes L2 error of dropped output vs input < 1e-7
Drop-rate counter incremented Matches manually counted BitSet popcount
capacity_factor=1.25 benchmark Overhead vs no-drop < 5% on 1024-token batch, 8 experts
Memory: per-expert capacity buffer ≤ Capacity · d_model · sizeof(f32) bytes allocated
cargo clippy -p moe-dispatch Zero warnings

3.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture file Purpose Expected output
fixtures/cap/uniform_load.json All experts receive exactly Capacity tokens dropped empty; per_expert_counts all = Capacity
fixtures/cap/overflow_expert0.json Expert 0 receives 2× Capacity requests pops = n_tokens − (Capacity·num_experts/K); counts[0]==Capacity
fixtures/cap/all_route_one_expert.json Pathological: all tokens → expert 0, K=1 counts[0]=Capacity; all others dropped

Regression: golden BitSet bytes stored in fixture; CI compares byte-for-byte.

3.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal Recovery
Non-deterministic drop order BitSet differs across runs Token iteration not fixed (HashMap/parallel iter) test_determinism flakes Force sequential TokenIdx-ascending iteration
Off-by-one in capacity formula Expert accepts Capacity+1 < vs <= in count check test_capacity_overflow fails at boundary Strict < (capacity exclusive upper bound)
Dropped token modified via accumulation Output ≠ input for dropped token Combine accumulates into zeroed buffer then adds residual L2 mismatch in residual test Init output to input; zero only non-dropped slots
BitSet size mismatch Panic/OOB BitSet built with wrong n_tokens OOB index in dispatch Pass n_tokens to BitSet ctor explicitly
capacity_factor NaN Capacity=0 (NaN compare) Unchecked f32 arithmetic upstream assertion in compute_capacity Validate f.is_finite() && f>0.0 at init
Per-expert count Vec wrong length Silent wrong drops num_experts mismatch config↔Vec telemetry counts off Assert len()==num_experts in debug
Residual doubles input in combine Output = 2×input for dropped residual added to non-zeroed buffer L2 ≫ 0 for dropped tokens Clear output buffer before combine

3.7 Performance Concerns

Concern Impact Mitigation
BitSet allocation per forward GC pressure at high QPS Arena-allocate; reset not reallocate
Stable-sort perm (O(n log n)) Dominant cost at small batches Counting sort (experts as buckets), O(n+E)
Scatter cache miss (non-contiguous access) D-cache thrash at large n_tokens Contiguous-per-expert layout from counting sort
Per-expert-drop AtomicU64 false sharing Cache-line bouncing, multi-core Pad entries to 64-byte alignment
Residual copy for dropped tokens Memcpy overhead Skip loop entirely if dropped.popcount()==0
CapacityFactor read path Not hot (once per forward) Cache computed Capacity in DispatchPlan

Target: build_dispatch_plan for 2048 tokens, 64 experts, K=2 completes in < 200 µs single-thread.

3.8 Edge Cases

Case Expected behavior
n_tokens=0 Empty DispatchPlan, no panic, dropped empty
capacity_factor>1.0 (slack) Capacity > expected load; zero drops under balanced routing
K=1 (top-1) Each token routes to exactly 1 expert; dropped if full
All tokens dropped Output = input tensor (full residual); drop_rate=1.0
num_experts=1 All tokens → expert 0; capacity=n_tokens·K; no drops
Integer overflow in capacity formula Saturate at usize::MAX, warn; effectively no limit
capacity_factor changed mid-batch Blocked by &mut self setter; no mid-forward race
Duplicate TokenIdx in routing decisions Debug assertion failure; validated at gate boundary

3.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Wrong capacity formula test failure; prod: silent token loss all tokens at that layer CI fixture overflow_expert0.json fix formula; redeploy
BitSet corruption (unsafe FFI) non-deterministic output wrong layer output, accuracy degrades cargo miri on dispatch; FFI audit remove unsafe, use safe BitSet crate
Drop rate spikes to 100% drop_rate_pct=100 output=input only; quality collapse alert drop_rate_pct>20% raise capacity_factor; shrink batch
Residual passthrough skipped dropped row = zero corrupted downstream zero rows test_residual_passthrough fix combine logic; re-run fixture
Per-expert count overflow (u32) wraps at 4B tokens capacity check passes erroneously assert count < 2^24 in debug u64 counts, or init-time max-batch assert

Postmortem: always log capacity_factor, n_tokens, K, num_experts, drop_rate in a structured span per forward, to enable reproduction.

3.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: moe_token_drop_rate (gauge, per-layer, alert >0.05), moe_per_expert_drop_count (counter vec, label=expert_id), moe_capacity_value (gauge, emitted once per config change).
  • Tracing: span dispatch::build_plan (children capacity_computed, drop_count); span dispatch::combine (child residual_passthrough_count).
  • Logging: WARN if drop_rate>0.10, ERROR if >0.50 (quality at risk); DEBUG logs capacity+n_tokens per forward.
  • Deployment/rollback: capacity_factor is a LayerConfig field, changeable via set_capacity_factor(f) at reload time (exclusive borrow, no hot-path overhead); no persistent state to migrate (stateless drop policy); DispatchPlan is internal, no serialization format to version in Phase 3.

16. Phase 4 — Load balancing — importance/load auxiliary loss, router z-loss, expert-utilization + routing-entropy metrics

4.1 Objectives

Objective Constraint
Importance-based auxiliary loss L_aux = CV(importance)² Matches Switch Transformer §4
Load auxiliary loss L_load = CV(load)² Matches GShard §4; differentiable via smooth load estimate
Router z-loss L_z = mean(log(Σexp(logits))²) ST-MoE §3; stabilizes logit scale, no capacity constraint
AuxLossState accumulates importance, load, z_loss per forward Returned alongside DispatchPlan; backprop by caller
Expert utilization + routing entropy metrics emitted every forward moe-telemetry; not inline with compute
Loss coefficients (alpha, beta, gamma) runtime-configurable Via RouterConfig, no recompile

Scope: moe-gate (loss computation), moe-core (AuxLossState), moe-telemetry (utilization+entropy); no moe-dispatch changes. Constraints: losses computed in f32 with logsumexp stability for z-loss; auxiliary losses do NOT feed back into top-k selection within one forward (guide parameter updates only); AuxLossState fields are Tensor handles for backprop, with scalars exposed separately for logging.

4.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P1 (Shazeer 2017 aux importance loss), P2 (GShard smooth load estimate), P5 (Zoph 2022 z-loss), I8 (aux loss monotonicity — α>0), I9 (gate logit safety — z-loss stabilizes scale), ADR-11 (tracing aux_loss spans)

moe-core/aux_loss.rs

struct AuxLossState { importance: Tensor, load: Tensor, z_loss: Tensor, total: Tensor }
  // importance/load: [num_experts] f32; z_loss/total: scalar f32
  // total = alpha*L_aux_imp + beta*L_aux_load + gamma*L_z
scalars (logging, no grad): importance_cv: f32, load_cv: f32, z_loss_scalar: f32

moe-gate/loss.rs (new) — compute_importance(softmax_weights) -> Tensor: sum softmax weights over tokens per expert → [E]. compute_load(topk_onehot) -> Tensor: smooth differentiable load estimate (Tutel-style). cv_squared(x) -> Tensor: (std(x)/mean(x))², guard mean<1e-8→0. compute_z_loss(logits) -> Tensor: logsumexp per token, squared, mean. build_aux_loss(cfg, logits, softmax_w, topk_onehot) -> AuxLossState. (P1, P5)

Smooth load estimate (differentiable, GShard Appendix): load[e] = mean_tokens(P(token selects e) · 1[token routes to e]), where P = softmax(logits)[token,e] carries gradient and the indicator is stop-grad. (P2)

moe-gate/router.rs — after topk_and_softmax, call build_aux_loss; attach to new wrapper GateOutput{routing: Vec<RoutingDecision>, aux: AuxLossState}.

moe-gate/lib.rs — RouterConfig gains aux_loss_alpha/aux_loss_beta/z_loss_gamma: f32 (defaults 1e-2, 1e-2, 1e-3); validated ∈[0.0,1.0], warn if >0.1 (destabilizing).

moe-telemetry/metrics.rs — expert_utilization[e]=load[e] (gauge vec); routing_entropy = -Σ_e p_e·log(p_e) with p_e=mean_tokens(softmax[*,e]) (scalar gauge); aux_loss_total (detached scalar gauge).

Tests — test_uniform_routing_low_cv (uniform → CV≈0, L_aux≈0), test_collapsed_routing_high_cv (all→expert0 → CV≫0), test_z_loss_numerical (vs reference logsumexp, tol 1e-6), test_gradient_flow (finite-diff on z_loss/L_aux_load vs logits), test_config_coefficients (alpha=0 → total=beta·L_load+gamma·L_z).

4.3 Deliverable

  • moe-gate::router returns GateOutput{routing, aux: AuxLossState}; AuxLossState.total is a Tensor ready for .backward() by the caller's training loop.
  • moe-telemetry publishes expert_utilization, routing_entropy, aux_loss_total each forward; moe-cli --dump-stats shows per-expert utilization + entropy.
  • All loss-formula tests pass; gradient-flow check passes for differentiable load.

4.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
test_uniform_routing_low_cv L_aux < 1e-4 for uniform load
test_collapsed_routing_high_cv L_aux > 0.5 when all tokens to 1 expert
test_z_loss_numerical Abs error vs reference < 1e-6 per element
test_gradient_flow Finite-diff / autodiff agreement < 1e-4
routing_entropy gauge Equals log(num_experts) for perfectly uniform routing
Expert utilization sum Σ_e utilization[e] ≈ K (each token contributes K selections)
Overhead vs Phase 2 Forward-pass overhead for aux-loss computation < 3%
cargo test -p moe-gate All pass; no panics on edge-case fixtures

4.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected
fixtures/loss/uniform_routing.json All experts receive equal load importance_cv<0.01, load_cv<0.01, entropy≈log(E)
fixtures/loss/collapsed_expert0.json All tokens → expert 0 importance_cv>1.0, load_cv>1.0
fixtures/loss/z_loss_reference.json Known logits, reference logsumexp z_loss_scalar matches golden float
fixtures/loss/coeff_zero_alpha.json alpha=0, beta=1, gamma=0 total=L_load; L_imp component absent
fixtures/loss/malformed_nan_logits.json NaN in gate logits MoeError::NanDetected from logsumexp guard
