fixtures/loss/replay_grad.json Gradient replay, finite-diff regression stored grad tensors match within 1e-4

4.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug Recovery
CV denominator near zero NaN in L_aux mean(importance)<1e-8, experts unused NaN gauge in telemetry guard: mean<eps → 0
Z-loss without logsumexp trick Inf for large logits Σexp(logits) overflows f32 z_loss_scalar=inf max + log(Σexp(x−max))
Load estimate not differentiable through indicator Zero gradient through load loss hard stop-grad missing/misplaced grad-flow test fails stop-grad indicator only, keep grad through softmax P
Loss coefficients too large Loss dominates task loss, routing collapses alpha>0.1 early in training aux_loss_total ≫ task loss default alpha=1e-2 + validation warning
Importance sum ≠ n_tokens·K wrong reduction axis summed over wrong axis sum-check assertion verify sum over tokens, not experts
Routing entropy log(0) NaN entropy expert with zero utilization NaN in telemetry guard p_e·log(p_e+eps)
Gradient explosion via z-loss loss spikes early logits not small-initialized aux_loss_total growing cap logit magnitude or reduce gamma
AuxLossState not detached for logging training-graph accumulation .item() reads hold graph memory grows per step .detach() before telemetry extraction

4.7 Performance Concerns

Concern Impact Mitigation
Softmax computed twice (routing + importance) 2× compute for soft weights reuse routing-pass softmax output
cv_squared reduction over E negligible at E=8–128 scalar ops, no optimization needed
Z-loss logsumexp over n_tokens O(n_tokens·E) intermediate memory fused row-max-then-sum pass, no re-materialized softmax
AuxLossState tensor allocation per-forward heap alloc pre-allocate in layer struct, reuse via Tensor::fill_
High-frequency telemetry writes cache-line contention on AtomicF32 write utilization array once per forward, no per-token atomic
Entropy computation (log ops) slow if not vectorized batch log over [E], SIMD/BLAS

4.8 Edge Cases

Case Expected
num_experts=1 all losses=0, entropy=0, CV guard returns 0
K=num_experts (dense) utilization=1.0 each; aux loss penalizes weight imbalance only
n_tokens=1 CV undefined (std of 1-elem=0) → return 0
alpha=beta=gamma=0 AuxLossState.total=0 tensor, no gradient from aux path
Mixed NaN/valid logits NaN propagates through logsumexp → MoeError::NanDetected
capacity_factor changed between forwards no effect — aux loss depends only on logits/routing decisions
Large logit magnitude (>20) logsumexp stable (f32 max≈3.4e38, exp(20)≈5e8)
Gradient through dropped tokens contribute 0 to load (indicator=0); loss unaffected by drop

4.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
NaN in aux loss aux_loss_total=NaN, telemetry alert training divergence guards in cv_squared/z_loss/log → MoeError fix logit init; reduce coefficients
Routing collapse (all→1 expert) entropy→0, load_cv→max accuracy degrades, latency spikes (capacity overflow) alert entropy<0.1·log(E) raise alpha/beta; check weight init
Loss gradient not backpropagated aux computed but not added to total imbalance grows unchecked test grad of total w.r.t. gate weights nonzero fix caller to add aux to task loss
Telemetry tensor holds compute graph memory leak per step OOM after N steps monitor RSS; moe_aux_loss_total stale .detach() before telemetry storage
CV overflow (large expert counts) CV≫1, aux loss spikes gradient explosion assert importance[e]≤n_tokens normalize importance by n_tokens before CV

Postmortem: structured log of importance_cv, load_cv, z_loss_scalar, routing_entropy every forward, enabling offline replay/diagnosis.

4.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: moe_expert_utilization (gauge vec/expert_id, alert >0.95 or <0.01), moe_routing_entropy (nats, max=log(E), alert <0.5·max), moe_aux_loss_total (detached scalar, alert >1.0), moe_importance_cv/moe_load_cv (diagnostic, >0.5 = imbalance).
  • Tracing: span gate::aux_loss (children importance_computed, load_computed, z_loss_computed); alert if duration >10% of gate time.
  • Logging: INFO active coefficients on first forward; WARN if entropy <0.3·log(E) sustained 100 forwards; DEBUG full importance/load tensors (disable in production).
  • Deployment: coefficients adjustable via layer.set_router_config(cfg) (exclusive borrow); no persistent state, rollback is config-only; GateOutput change (adds aux) version-gated behind aux-loss feature flag; inference sets inference_mode: bool to zero all loss coefficients (z-loss unneeded at inference).

17. Phase 5 — Batched expert compute — group tokens per expert, batched/grouped GEMM (FFI), ragged batch + padding, FFN (GEMM->act->GEMM)

5.1 Objectives

Objective Constraint
Expert FFN: h = act(GEMM(w_in, x)); out = GEMM(w_out, h) w_in∈[d_ff,d_model], w_out∈[d_model,d_ff]; activation=SiLU/GeLU
Group tokens per expert into one contiguous batch, single GEMM call Contiguous layout from Phase-2 permutation
Batched GEMM via BLAS/CUDA FFI unsafe isolated in moe-expert/gemm_ffi.rs
Ragged batches: experts receive 0..Capacity tokens No token-dim padding beyond alignment; use per-expert count from DispatchPlan
Padding for GEMM alignment (multiple of 16) Padding rows discarded before combine; never enter dropped BitSet
Per-expert GEMM timing exposed to telemetry Nanosecond wall-clock via Instant, no perf-event

Scope: moe-expert (FFN+FFI), moe-core (ExpertWeights), moe-dispatch (permuted input buffers); no moe-gate changes. Constraints: MSRV 1.78, no nightly SIMD; FFI target CBLAS cblas_sgemm (CPU), optional cuBLAS behind cuda feature; ExpertWeights pre-loaded into arena (no per-forward alloc); backward pass is caller's responsibility — Phase 5 is forward-only.

5.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P6 (Megablocks grouped GEMM layout), I7 (weight shard completeness), ADR-04 (cblas-sys+cudarc FFI), ADR-05 (Arena scratch), P7 (FFI safety — unsafe confined to gemm_ffi.rs), ADR-09 (safetensors loading), P10 (correctness before optimization — ref vs BLAS)

moe-core/expert_weights.rs

struct ExpertWeights { w_in: Tensor, w_out: Tensor, bias_in: Option<Tensor>, bias_out: Option<Tensor>, expert_id: ExpertId }
  // w_in [d_ff,d_model], w_out [d_model,d_ff], bias_in [d_ff], bias_out [d_model], all row-major f32

Stored Arc<ExpertWeights>, shared across forwards, no mutation post-load. ExpertWeights::load(path, cfg) -> Result<Self>: mmap or arena copy, layout-validated. (I7, ADR-09)

moe-expert/gemm_ffi.rs (unsafe boundary) — unsafe fn sgemm(m,n,k,alpha,A,lda,B,ldb,beta,C,ldc) wraps cblas_sgemm; #[cfg(feature="cuda")] unsafe fn cublas_sgemm(...) wraps cuBLAS. All unsafe confined here; moe-expert/ffn.rs callers use safe wrappers only. (P7, ADR-04) moe-expert/ffn.rs — fn expert_ffn(tokens: &Tensor, weights: &ExpertWeights, activation: Activation, scratch: &mut ScratchBuffer) -> Tensor: pad n_tok to n_tok_padded=align_up(n_tok, 16) (zero rows); h=GEMM(tokens_padded, w_in^T) into scratch; activation in-place over i<n_tok only (padding rows skipped); out_padded=GEMM(h, w_out^T); return view out_padded[0..n_tok,*] (no copy). (P6)

moe-expert/lib.rs — fn run_all_experts(dispatch, input_permuted, weights, cfg, telemetry) -> Tensor: alloc [total_assigned_tokens,d_model] from arena; for each expert e, n_tok=per_expert_counts[e] (skip GEMM if 0), slice input/output at running offset, time via Instant, call expert_ffn, telemetry.record_expert_gemm_ns(e, elapsed), write into output slice; return assembled buffer. moe-expert/scratch.rs — struct ScratchBuffer{buf: Vec<f32>, capacity_rows, d_ff}, fn ensure(&mut self, n_rows) (grow-only), fn as_slice_mut(&mut self, n_rows) -> &mut [f32]; one per thread, reused across experts within a forward. (ADR-05) moe-telemetry: per_expert_gemm_ns: Vec<AtomicU64>, gemm_skip_count: AtomicU64, padding_tokens_total: AtomicU64. Tests — test_single_expert_ffn (n_tok=8/d_model=64/d_ff=256 vs numpy golden), test_zero_token_expert (n_tok=0 → skip, no panic), test_ragged_batch (counts [3,7,0,5] vs per-expert reference), test_padding_discarded (output rows = real tokens only, no padding), test_weight_immutability (concurrent Arc reads, no race), test_activation_silu/test_activation_gelu (100 random points vs reference).

5.3 Deliverable

  • run_all_experts executes all experts for a batch, output matches per-expert reference; GEMM FFI calls CBLAS, feature=cuda compiles+links cuBLAS (CI: CPU only).
  • ScratchBuffer reused across experts, zero per-expert heap allocation in hot path; telemetry (per-expert latency, skip count, padding waste) visible via moe-cli --dump-stats; cargo test -p moe-expert all pass on CPU.

5.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
test_single_expert_ffn Max abs error vs numpy reference < 1e-5
test_ragged_batch All expert outputs within 1e-5 of per-expert reference
test_zero_token_expert No panic; gemm_skip_count incremented
test_padding_discarded Output has exactly Σ per_expert_counts rows
GEMM throughput (512 tok, d_model=1024, d_ff=4096) ≥ 50 GFLOPS on 8-core CPU (MKL/OpenBLAS)
ScratchBuffer reuse Zero allocations from 2nd run_all_experts call onward
cargo clippy -p moe-expert -- -D warnings Clean
Unsafe surface Confined to gemm_ffi.rs; #![forbid(unsafe_code)] elsewhere in crate

5.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected
fixtures/expert/single_ffn_f32.npz n_tok=8, d_model=64, d_ff=256, SiLU max abs error < 1e-5
fixtures/expert/ragged_batch_4experts.json counts [3,7,0,5] all within 1e-5 of per-expert reference
fixtures/expert/zero_token_all_experts.json all counts=0 empty output, no panic, skip_count=num_experts
fixtures/expert/gelu_activation.npz GeLU reference max abs error < 1e-5
fixtures/expert/large_batch_timing.json 2048 tok, 64 experts, K=2 timing metadata; CI asserts < 5s CPU
fixtures/expert/malformed_weight_shape.json w_in shape mismatch MoeError::ShapeMismatch at load
fixtures/expert/replay_scratch_reuse.json two consecutive forward calls 2nd call zero allocations, identical outputs
fixtures/expert/padding_alignment.json n_tok=17, align=16→pad 32 output rows=17, no padding rows leak out

5.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug Recovery
Padding rows included in output wrong shape, combine reads garbage returned slice includes padding test_padding_discarded fails slice to [0..n_tok,*] before return
GEMM leading-dimension mismatch silent wrong results row/col-major mismatch in lda/ldb output vs reference diverges silently enforce row-major throughout, assert shapes
Expert weight aliasing wrong output for some experts weights[e] Arc pointing to shared storage concurrent read/write check distinct ExpertWeights per expert; assert expert_id matches slot
ScratchBuffer not grown OOB write ensure not called before use segfault/corrupted output always scratch.ensure(n_tok_padded) before expert_ffn
Zero-count expert not skipped GEMM on empty slice → panic/UB missing n_tok==0 guard test_zero_token_expert fails explicit guard, tested
Activation applied to padding rows GEMM2 produces wrong output activation loop runs full padded range padded vs unpadded output differ activation loop bounded 0..n_tok only
CBLAS not linked link error at build time missing links in build.rs undefined symbol at build cargo:rustc-link-lib=openblas in build.rs
GEMM timing overhead dominates small experts profiling noise Instant::now() called O(num_experts) times timing variance analysis gate behind telemetry feature; batch timing

5.7 Performance Concerns

Concern Impact Mitigation
Serial expert loop linear in num_experts, single thread Phase 5 single-threaded; parallelized via moe-sched in Phase 7
GEMM for small n_tok (<16) low utilization, overhead dominates pad to GEMM_ALIGN; fused micro-kernel explored in Phase 7
ScratchBuffer peak = max_count·d_ff·4 per-thread memory pre-allocate to Capacity·d_ff·4, known at layer init
Output buffer allocation per forward Arena fragmentation pre-allocate output slab, reset per forward
Weight cache thrash [d_ff,d_model] L3 miss at large d_ff fits LLC at d_model=1024/d_ff=4096 (16 MB); larger needs tiling (Phase 7)
In-place activation vs separate buffer aliasing risk with FFI ensure h buffer not aliased by GEMM output pre-activation
GEMM leading-dimension alignment misaligned ld reduces BLAS perf pad weight row stride to 64-byte boundary at load
Instant::now() in hot loop ~20ns × num_experts one Instant::now() before loop, per-expert delta

Target: 512 tokens, 8 experts, d_model=1024, d_ff=4096 → run_all_experts < 50 ms CPU (OpenBLAS).

5.8 Edge Cases

Case Expected
n_tok=1 for all experts valid, GEMM M=1 (padded to 16), correct output
d_ff=0 MoeError::InvalidConfig at ExpertWeights::load
num_experts=0 MoeError::InvalidConfig at layer init
Capacity=0 all tokens dropped; all-zero counts; empty output
Activation=None (linear expert) identity between the two GEMMs
Weight dtype=f16 Phase 5 f32-only → MoeError::UnsupportedDtype (f16 deferred to Phase 8)
Concurrent reads of same ExpertWeights safe via Arc, read-only post-load
ScratchBuffer shared across threads undefined; type is !Send, compiler enforces single-thread
CBLAS error (void return, no error code) wrap with pre/post NaN assertion on output

5.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Silent GEMM wrong result (lda mismatch) output diverges, test failure all expert outputs wrong reference fixture CI comparison shape assertion before every FFI call
OOB write in ScratchBuffer segfault/memory corruption process crash ASAN in CI (--features asan) ensure+bounds check before write
CBLAS unavailable at runtime dlopen/link failure no expert compute possible startup ExpertWeights::verify_blas() fall back to naive Rust GEMM (correctness only)
Expert weight file corrupted wrong output, checksum mismatch model inference wrong SHA-256 checksum at load reload weights, alert operator
ScratchBuffer grows unbounded memory spike, outlier batch OOM cap at max_capacity·d_ff·4, MoeError::OOM reduce batch size / increase memory
NaN in GEMM output propagates through activation+combine layer output NaN, downstream diverges post-GEMM NaN check (debug), moe_nan_count reload weights, or fix upstream gate

Postmortem: structured log per call: {expert_id, n_tok, gemm_ns, output_nan, scratch_peak_bytes}.

5.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: moe_expert_gemm_ns (histogram/expert_id, p50/p99), moe_expert_gemm_skip_count (routing imbalance signal), moe_padding_waste_tokens (GEMM_ALIGN tuning signal), moe_scratch_peak_bytes (memory ceiling gauge).
  • Tracing: span expert::run_all wraps run_all_experts; child span per expert expert::ffn[{id}] (GEMM1/activation/GEMM2 events); attributes n_tok, n_tok_padded, d_ff.
  • Logging: DEBUG per-expert n_tok+duration (off in prod); WARN on output_nan_check trigger; ERROR if ScratchBuffer::ensure exceeds max_scratch_bytes.
  • Deployment: MOE_BLAS_BACKEND env var (openblas/mkl/accelerate) resolved via build.rs; feature flags cuda, telemetry (default on), debug-nan-check (default off); ExpertWeights loaded once at server start (no runtime reload in Phase 5); rollback = redeploy previous binary (no persistent state); weight format is versioned custom binary, incompatible version → MoeError::FormatVersion; run_all_experts signature stable Phases 5–7 (Phase-7 moe-sched wraps without API change).

18. Phase 6 — Expert parallelism (multi-process) — EP process groups, expert-weight sharding, all-to-all dispatch + combine across ranks

6.1 Objectives

Goal Constraint
Introduce EpGroup; assign experts to ranks by e % world_size Must compose with existing single-rank dispatch path
Shard ExpertWeights so rank r holds experts {e: e%W==r} Weight tensors must not replicate across EP ranks
Wire plan → a2a send → remote expert exec → a2a recv → combine Token conservation invariant preserved end-to-end
Support world_size=1 (no-op EP, all experts local) as identity case Single-rank path must not regress latency or correctness
Expose EpGroup::barrier()/all_reduce() for aux-loss sync Aux/z-loss aggregation across ranks required for stable training

Scope: moe-comm owns transport+collectives; moe-expert adds sharding; moe-runtime wires cross-rank forward. No HTTP/REST — in-proc shared-memory and multi-proc MPI/NCCL transports only. Constraints: all ranks enter a2a simultaneously (stragglers stall globally); per_expert_counts communicated before payload a2a; CapacityFactor is per-rank, global counts used only for logging.

6.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P2 (GShard a2a layout), I4 (EP ownership consistency), I10 (A2A symmetric counts), I11 (single-rank short-circuit), ADR-08 (MPI/NCCL/InProc transport), ADR-06 (tokio async), P9 (EP consistency)

moe-comm/src/ep_group.rs — EpGroup{rank, world_size, transport: Transport, pg_id: ProcessGroupId}; Transport = InProc(Arc<SharedMemArena>)|Mpi(MpiComm)|Nccl(NcclComm); EpGroup::new validates rank<world_size; owner_of(ExpertId)->u32 = id%world_size *(I4)*; local_experts(num_experts) filters by owner; barrier() (post weight-init); all_reduce_f32(buf, op) for aux-loss accumulation.

moe-expert/src/sharding.rs — ExpertWeights::load_shard(config, ep_group) -> Result<ShardedWeights> loads only local experts (panics on unexpected experts in checkpoint); ShardedWeights{local: HashMap<ExpertId,ExpertWeights>, ep_group}; invariant local.keys() == ep_group.local_experts(...); validated shape/dtype/GEMM-stride alignment per expert on load.

moe-dispatch/src/ep_dispatch.rs — exchange_counts(plan, ep) -> EpCountTable{send_counts[W], recv_counts[W]} bucketed by owning rank via all_to_all_u32 (small synchronous payload, I10); ep_scatter(tokens, plan, ep) -> Vec<TensorShard> partitions by destination rank; ep_gather(shards, ep) -> Tensor reassembles from a2a receive buffers.

moe-comm/src/collectives.rs — all_to_all_v(ep, send_buf, send_counts, recv_buf, recv_counts) dispatches on Transport: InProc → shared-mem ring; MPI → MPI_Alltoallv; NCCL → ncclAllToAll (2.7+). all_reduce_f32(ep, buf, Sum) wraps transport sum-reduce. All collectives annotate span comm.alltoall with bytes_sent/bytes_recv/rank/pg_id. (ADR-08)

moe-runtime/src/ep_forward.rs — ep_forward(input, gate_out, plan, sharded, ep, sched) -> Result<Tensor>: ep_scatter → exchange_counts (recv sizing) → a2a dispatch payload → local expert FFN on received tokens → a2a combine results → ep_gather → weighted combine with gate weights (dropped tokens: residual passthrough). (P2)

Tests (moe-comm/tests/ep_integration.rs) — spawn W threads each owning a synthetic EpGroup rank (InProc); verify round-trip token count/content/order; verify owner_of bijection covers all experts; stress: 1000-round random dispatch+combine vs reference dense path.

6.3 Deliverable

  • EpGroup compiles, passes unit tests for all transport variants (InProc mandatory, MPI/NCCL feature-gated); ShardedWeights::load_shard correctly partitions experts, panics on shape mismatch.
  • 4-rank/8-expert/512-token InProc integration test matches single-rank reference (atol 1e-5); moe-cli --ep-world-size 4 --ep-rank 0..3 launches cleanly, telemetry logs per-rank utilization; operators inspect rank/pg_id/send_counts/recv_counts via tracing spans.

6.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
cargo test -p moe-comm ep_ 100% pass
Token conservation, 4-rank InProc count_in == count_out per TokenIdx
Numerical diff vs dense reference (atol 1e-5, rtol 1e-4) passes for world_size ∈ {1,2,4,8}
a2a metadata exchange latency (8 ranks, 1K tok, InProc) < 50 µs
ep_forward round-trip (4 ranks, 512 tok, 8 experts, InProc) < 5 ms wall-clock
Per-token allocation inside ep_scatter/ep_gather none (verified via dhat heap profiler)
all_reduce_f32 sum (8 ranks, 1024 floats) vs serial sum exact match
world_size=1 path calls any collective none (mock transport assertion)

6.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/ep/world1_identity.bin world_size=1 no-op byte-identical to single-rank path
fixtures/ep/world4_sym_8exp.bin 4 ranks, 8 experts, k=2, 512 tok token conservation + atol 1e-5 vs reference
fixtures/ep/count_exchange_4r.bin EpCountTable round-trip, all ranks send_counts[r]==recv_counts[r]
fixtures/ep/oob_expert_id.bin ExpertId ≥ num_experts in routing MoeError::RoutingOutOfBounds
fixtures/ep/wrong_shard.bin checkpoint has wrong expert set for rank panic "shard_mismatch"
fixtures/ep/replay_baseline_v1.bin locked regression output byte-identical across refactors
fixtures/ep/capacity1_drop.bin capacity_factor=0.1 forces drops dropped tokens == input slice

6.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Debug/Fix
Count exchange skipped before payload recv_buf under/over-allocated, silent corruption/OOM log recv_counts[*], compare vs peer send_counts; recv_counts must come from global exchange, not local plan alone
Non-symmetric a2a deadlock/truncation in MPI assert symmetry in InProc transport (off-by-one in owner_of or unzeroed drop counts); drain+re-init EpGroup
Weight shard not zero-copied 2× peak memory (loads all, drops non-local) seek to expert offsets directly, ShardedWeights::load_partial
Barrier after init missing forward proceeds on uninitialized weights → NaN EpGroup::barrier() after weight load; log rank arrival order
Aux-loss all_reduce scope creep comm overhead dominates at high frequency accumulate locally N steps, all_reduce once per macro-batch
InProc lock contention (Arc<Mutex<VecDeque>>) high contention at world_size≥8 lock-free ArrayQueue SPSC, split send/recv queues
ExpertId owner mismatch across ranks integer overflow at num_experts near u32::MAX/W saturating_add, panic if num_experts≥u32::MAX

6.7 Performance Concerns

Concern Impact Mitigation
ep_scatter allocation per call malloc count pre-allocate TensorShard pool in Scheduler, reuse across micro-batches
Count-exchange latency µs per collective synchronous all_to_all_u32 (tiny payload), pipeline with plan build
Intra-node a2a (InProc) bytes/µs shared-memory ring, raw f32 copy, no serialize/deserialize
Expert GEMM load imbalance across ranks utilization variance load-balance aux loss; monitor per-rank utilization
all_reduce for aux loss frequency accumulate N micro-batches, reduce once (N configurable)
Barrier at weight init wall-clock async pre-fetch, barrier after IO complete, parallel shard loads
HashMap<ExpertId,ExpertWeights> lookup per token cache miss Vec<Option<ExpertWeights>> indexed by local expert ordinal

6.8 Edge Cases

  • world_size=1: ep_scatter/ep_gather are identity, no collective issued; must pass Phase-4 single-rank tests. num_experts<world_size: some ranks hold zero experts, recv_counts[r]=0 valid, must not deadlock.
  • All tokens dropped (capacity_factor→0): send_counts all-zero, a2a still executes with zero-byte payload, output=input (residual).
  • Rank crash mid-a2a (MPI): remaining ranks block — detected via MPI_Wtick-based watchdog timeout → MoeError::EpRankTimeout(rank); recovery: abort group, restart from checkpoint.
  • Weight checkpoint missing experts for rank → Err(MoeError::ShardMissing), process must not continue. d_model mismatch sender/receiver → caught in count-exchange validation before payload send.
  • Concurrent forward calls on same EpGroup: unsupported — single active collective slot, enforced via Mutex/semaphore.

6.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
EP rank timeout watchdog >5s on collective all EP ranks stall MoeError::EpRankTimeout; abort group, reload shard, rejoin
Weight shard corrupt shape/dtype mismatch on load single rank, all its experts re-download checkpoint shard
a2a payload corruption (InProc) token count mismatch post-gather wrong expert outputs, silent degradation assertions in ep_gather; re-init shared-mem arena
Aux-loss all_reduce divergence loss NaN across ranks post-reduce training instability is_nan() check, skip update, re-sync last known-good loss
ExpertId OOB (malformed routing) owner_of panics/wraps single forward pass aborted catch in ep_scatter, MoeError::RoutingOutOfBounds, reject batch

Postmortem signals: moe_ep_rank_stalls_total, moe_ep_payload_mismatch_total, moe_ep_shard_load_errors_total.

6.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: moe_ep_send_bytes{rank,pg_id}, moe_ep_recv_bytes{rank,pg_id}, moe_ep_alltoall_latency_us{pg_id}, moe_ep_expert_utilization{expert_id}, moe_ep_rank_stalls_total.
  • Tracing: ep_group.scatter (send_counts), ep_group.gather (recv_counts), ep_group.all_reduce (op, bytes), ep_group.barrier.
  • Logging: INFO EpGroup init (rank, world_size, transport, local experts); WARN zero-expert rank; ERROR shard mismatch.
  • Deployment/rollback: world_size must match checkpoint shard count (validated at startup via validate_coverage); power-of-2 world_size reduction is safe with matching reshard, non-power-of-2 requires full reshard; EpGroup serializes pg_id+rank+world_size into checkpoint metadata (version-checked, breaking bump needs migration tool); InProc safe for single-machine multi-GPU, MPI needs mpirun, NCCL needs ncclCommInitRank bootstrap.

19. Phase 7 — All-to-all communication engine — transport abstraction, pairwise/ring a2a, serialization, flow control + backpressure

7.1 Objectives

Goal Constraint
Transport trait with InProc, MPI, NCCL backends feature-gated (mpi, nccl); InProc always compiled
Variable-length all-to-all (all_to_all_v), per-rank send/recv counts supports token tensors (f32/bf16) and u32 metadata
Pairwise a2a reference impl + ring a2a optimized impl pairwise for correctness testing; ring for production
Backpressure via bounded channel queue, no unbounded buffering protects fast-sender/slow-receiver; max-in-flight configurable
Serialization: raw f32/bf16 memcpy, dtype tag in header header: magic, dtype, shape, rank_from, rank_to
Per-transfer span + metrics for bytes/latency/queue depth no silent drops; every blocked send emits a metric

Scope: moe-comm crate only; upper layers interact solely via Transport trait, never backend-specific types; transport is stateless per-collective, EpGroup owns the instance.

7.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-08 (MPI/NCCL/InProc transport trait), I10 (A2A symmetric counts), I11 (single-rank short-circuit), P5 (explicit backpressure), P9 (EP consistency — owner_of), ADR-06 (tokio async wrapper, Phase 8), ADR-07 (crossbeam bounded SPSC, InProc)

moe-comm/src/transport.rs

pub trait Transport: Send + Sync {
    fn all_to_all_v(&self, ep: &EpGroup, send: &[u8], send_counts: &[u32], send_displs: &[u32],
        recv: &mut [u8], recv_counts: &[u32], recv_displs: &[u32]) -> Result<(), MoeError>;
    fn barrier(&self, ep: &EpGroup) -> Result<(), MoeError>;
    fn all_reduce_f32(&self, ep: &EpGroup, buf: &mut [f32], op: ReduceOp) -> Result<(), MoeError>;
}

send_displs/recv_displs are byte offsets (counts·dtype_size, I10); all methods block until collective completes (async wrapper added in Phase 8, ADR-06).

moe-comm/src/inproc.rs — InProcTransport{channels: Arc<Vec<Vec<Sender<Message>>>>}, channels[src][dst] a bounded crossbeam::channel (QUEUE_DEPTH=4 default). all_to_all_v: send to each peer r in rank order (send[send_displs[r]..] as a Message), then receive from each peer with 10s timeout, copy into recv[recv_displs[r]..]. Backpressure is natural (bounded send() blocks on full queue); barrier via Arc<AtomicUsize> countdown + Condvar. moe-comm/src/pairwise_a2a.rs — PairwiseA2A::execute: W·(W−1) point-to-point transfers, self-to-self via direct memcpy; correctness-harness only, not production path. moe-comm/src/ring_a2a.rs — W steps; step s: rank r sends to (r+s)%W, receives from (r-s+W)%W; pipelined where transport allows concurrent ops; chunked at RING_CHUNK_BYTES=65536; self-to-self step is memcpy only. moe-comm/src/mpi_transport.rs (feature=mpi) — wraps rsmpi; all_to_all_v→MPI_Alltoallv, barrier→MPI_Barrier, all_reduce_f32→MPI_Allreduce(SUM); errors → MoeError::CommError(mpi_errno). moe-comm/src/nccl_transport.rs (feature=nccl) — bindgen FFI to libnccl.so; ncclAllToAll for uniform a2a, ncclGroupStart/End grouped P2P for variable a2a; stream synchronized via cudaStreamSynchronize before return; errors → MoeError::NcclError. moe-comm/src/wire.rs — WireHeader{magic:[u8;4], dtype: u8, shape_len: u8, shape:[u32;4], from_rank: u32, to_rank: u32} (32B fixed); encode_tensor_slice/decode_header (validates magic); dtype 0=f32,1=bf16,2=u32, unknown → MoeError::UnknownDtype. Flow control — bounded InProc channels block sender on full queue; max_in_flight config bounds outstanding messages per (src,dst); moe_comm_send_blocked_count{src,dst} increments on every blocked send. (P5) Tests (moe-comm/tests/a2a_tests.rs) — test_pairwise_vs_ring (W∈{2,4,8}, byte-identical outputs), test_a2a_v_variable_counts (non-uniform counts, exact recv fill), test_backpressure (fill to depth, sender blocks), test_wire_roundtrip (every dtype, header+data identity).

7.3 Deliverable

  • Transport trait object compiles; InProc (mandatory), MPI/NCCL (feature-gated) all implement it; pairwise and ring a2a produce identical results for W∈{1,2,4,8} with random inputs.
  • Variable-length a2a test (asymmetric counts, e.g. rank 0→rank1:300, rank0→rank2:10) yields correct recv counts on all peers; wire encode/decode round-trips f32/bf16/u32 up to [1024,4096] with zero failures.
  • Backpressure demonstrable at queue depth=4 (sender blocks when receiver slow); moe-cli --transport inproc --a2a-algo ring runs synthetic benchmark reporting GB/s + mean latency.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
cargo test -p moe-comm 100% pass
Pairwise vs ring output diff byte-identical, all W, seeds 0..99
all_to_all_v variable counts recv_total==send_total bytes, all ranks
InProc a2a throughput (4 ranks, 1 MB total) > 2 GB/s
InProc a2a latency (4 ranks, 1 KB total) < 20 µs
Wire decode, malformed magic MoeError::BadMagic, no panic
Backpressure: sender blocks after queue full < 1 ms (timing assertion)
NCCL feature build (CUDA available) cargo build --features nccl clean

7.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Debug/Fix
Displacements in elements, not bytes recv buffer short-fills send_displs[r]=Σcounts[0..r]·dtype_size; assert total matches; MPI expects byte offsets
Ring a2a self-to-self sent via channel deadlock at channel depth=1 special-case peer==self_rank → direct memcpy
NCCL grouped P2P returns before stream completes flaky NaN in NCCL path always cudaStreamSynchronize after ncclGroupEnd
WireHeader not cache-line aligned perf regression (cache misses) enforce 8-byte alignment, pad to 32 bytes
Backpressure not propagated (dead receiver) thread leak, indefinite block send_timeout(10s) → MoeError::CommTimeout
Recv buffer sized wrong (caller bug) OOB write validate recv_buf.len()≥Σrecv_counts·dtype_size at entry, return Err
MPI communicator reused across overlapping EP groups non-deterministic tag conflicts MPI_Comm_dup per EpGroup

7.7 Performance Concerns

Concern Impact Mitigation
Per-message heap allocation allocations/s pre-allocated BumpArena per micro-batch, reused across a2a steps
Channel contention at high world_size lock-acquire µs crossbeam::channel lock-free MPMC over std::sync::mpsc
Ring a2a serialization per chunk µs/chunk zero-copy if contiguous, skip header for self-to-self
MPI eager threshold msg size vs rendezvous MPICH_EAGER_MAX=65536; large tensors use rendezvous
NCCL all-to-all bandwidth GB/s per link prefer NVLink over PCIe; monitor nccl_comm_bw
Synchronous-blocking stack depth thread stack ensure calling thread stack ≥256 KB
Recv buffer zero-init cycles pre-zero only in debug, skip in release

7.8 Edge Cases

  • world_size=1: all_to_all_v degenerates to self-memcpy (no channels), completes <1µs. All send_counts=0: zero-byte a2a valid, barrier still executes.
  • Asymmetric send_counts (some zero, some not): displacement math must handle zero-length slices without OOB.
  • Peer dies mid-receive (InProc) → Err(RecvError) mapped to MoeError::PeerDisconnected(rank). Dtype mismatch sender/receiver → decoder asserts, MoeError::DtypeMismatch.
  • recv_counts not pre-exchanged (caller bug): entry-point validation returns Err rather than undersized write.
  • Concurrent a2a on same EpGroup: unsupported, single active collective slot via Mutex<()>. NCCL stream still in-flight on next call: cudaStreamSynchronize on entry.

7.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Peer disconnect RecvError on channel single collective, all ranks stall MoeError::PeerDisconnected; restart EP group
Buffer OOB write size check at entry memory corruption in worker reject batch before write, log recv_counts
MPI error code rsmpi returns Err entire MPI job MoeError::CommError; abort + restart MPI world
NCCL stream error ncclResult_t≠Success all NCCL ranks in group MoeError::NcclError; reinit comm, rejoin
Wire decode failure magic/dtype check fails single message BadMagic/UnknownDtype; log sender, drop message

Postmortem signals: moe_comm_errors_total{kind}, moe_comm_timeout_total, moe_comm_peer_disconnected_total.

7.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: moe_comm_alltoall_bytes{rank,direction}, moe_comm_alltoall_latency_us, moe_comm_send_blocked_count{src,dst}, moe_comm_queue_depth{src,dst}, moe_comm_errors_total{kind}.
  • Tracing: comm.all_to_all_v span carries world_size, total_send_bytes, total_recv_bytes, transport, algorithm, duration.
  • Logging: DEBUG per-chunk (gated TRACE_COMM=1); INFO collective start/end; WARN send-blocked>1s; ERROR transport error.
  • Config (CommConfig): queue_depth, ring_chunk_bytes, send_timeout_secs, algorithm:{pairwise,ring,auto} (auto=ring if W≥4). Transport selection via --transport (InProc/MPI/NCCL).
  • Deployment: MPI needs mpirun -np W; NCCL needs NCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME for multi-node. WireHeader magic [0x4D,0x4F,0x45,0x41], extensible dtype, shape up to 4-D; breaking change = magic bump + deprecation window.
  • Debugging: RUST_LOG=moe_comm=trace for per-chunk transfers; --dump-a2a saves raw send/recv buffers for offline diff.

20. Phase 8 — Comm/compute overlap scheduler — streams/queues, double-buffering, micro-batch pipelining, prefetch

8.1 Objectives

Goal Constraint
Overlap a2a dispatch comm with expert GEMM compute of prior micro-batch needs ≥2 concurrent micro-batches in flight (double-buffering)
moe-sched crate: Scheduler owns stream pool, work queues, buffer pool no direct comm/GEMM calls from moe-runtime; all via Scheduler
Micro-batch pipelining: split input into M micro-batches M configurable (1=no overlap, 2=double-buffer, M>2 deeper pipeline)
Prefetch next micro-batch a2a while current expert runs requires non-blocking a2a submit (returns handle, not result)
Double-buffering: two CombineBuffer instances alternated ping-pong; never read+write same buffer concurrently
CUDA: GEMM/a2a on separate streams, event-based sync InProc path: thread-per-stage with channel handoff

Scope: moe-sched crate, consumed by moe-runtime::ep_forward; does NOT own GEMM (delegated to moe-expert) or transport (delegated to moe-comm) — pure orchestration layer.

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P8 (Tutel adaptive overlap + double-buffer sizing, comm/compute overlap explicit), I6 (overlap liveness — a2a/GEMM never deadlock), ADR-06 (tokio streams), ADR-07 (crossbeam bounded work queue), P5 (explicit backpressure), P13 (single writer per buffer — ping-pong)

moe-sched/src/scheduler.rs

pub struct Scheduler { stream_pool: StreamPool, work_queue: WorkQueue, buffer_pool: BufferPool, config: SchedConfig }
pub struct SchedConfig { num_micro_batches: usize, buffer_depth: usize, prefetch_depth: usize, stream_count: usize }
  // defaults: M=2, buffer_depth=2, prefetch_depth=1, stream_count=4

moe-sched/src/stream_pool.rs — StreamPool{streams: Vec<Stream>, event_pool: Vec<Event>}; Stream=Cuda(cudaStream_t)|Thread(Arc<WorkerThread>); Event=CudaEvent|Condvar; acquire()->StreamHandle (round-robin LRU, blocks if busy); record_event/wait_event (cudaStreamWaitEvent or condvar). moe-sched/src/work_queue.rs — WorkQueue on crossbeam::deque::Injector<WorkItem>; WorkItem=A2aDispatch{micro_batch_id, send_buf, ep}|ExpertCompute{micro_batch_id, input, weights}|A2aCombine{...}|Combine{micro_batch_id, recv_buf, routing}; workers steal via per-thread Stealer; bounded back-pressure channel caps M·2 in-flight items. (ADR-07, P5) moe-sched/src/buffer_pool.rs — BufferPool{slots: Vec<BufferSlot>, free: Mutex<Vec<BufferHandle>>}, BufferSlot{buf: CombineBuffer, in_use: AtomicBool}; acquire() spins on compare_exchange (max 10µs) then park_thread; release() clears in_use, unparks waiters; default pool size M·2 (ping-pong margin). (P13) moe-sched/src/pipeline.rs — MicroBatchPipeline::run(input, gate_out, ...) -> Result<Tensor>: split input into M equal parts (split_dim0); per i in 0..M, acquire ping-pong buffer i%2, submit A2aDispatch(i) on comm stream, submit ExpertCompute(i-1) on compute stream once i≥1 (overlaps with dispatch of i), record/wait events around combine; drain remaining stages; concatenate outputs. Invariant: buf(i%2) written by combine of i−2 must release before dispatch of i reuses it (enforced by acquire/release ordering). (I6) moe-sched/src/prefetch.rs — Prefetcher{lookahead, in_flight: Mutex<VecDeque<PrefetchHandle>>}; submit_a2a pushes A2aDispatch, returns handle; PrefetchHandle::wait()->RecvBuffer blocks until a2a completes; lookahead=1 → one a2a in flight while expert computes. moe-sched/src/cuda_sync.rs (feature=cuda) — submit_gemm/submit_a2a enqueue on separate CUDA/NCCL streams; record_event/wait_event (cudaStreamWaitEvent) ensures GEMM never starts before a2a recv completes. Tests (moe-sched/tests/pipeline_tests.rs) — test_double_buffer_no_overlap (M=1 correctness), test_double_buffer_overlap (M=2 output==M=1, wall-clock improvement>10%), test_buffer_pool_no_aliasing (disjoint memory), test_pipeline_M4 (4 micro-batches, no deadlock), test_prefetch_handle_wait (recv buffer populated).

8.3 Deliverable

  • Scheduler::new(config) compiles; InProc thread-based streams functional without CUDA; MicroBatchPipeline::run at M=2 matches M=1 serial output exactly (atol 0 integer tensors).
  • Wall-clock: M=2 vs M=1 (512 tok, 8 experts, InProc) ≥10% reduction in end-to-end forward time; buffer pool shows no aliasing across concurrent micro-batches (address-uniqueness assertion).
  • moe-cli --num-micro-batches 2 --prefetch-depth 1 logs overlap utilization; operators inspect moe_sched_overlap_fraction, moe_sched_buffer_pool_wait_us, per-stage latency histograms.

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
cargo test -p moe-sched 100% pass
M=2 output == M=1 output exact (int tensors); atol 1e-5 (f32)
M=2 wall-clock speedup vs M=1 (InProc, 512 tok) ≥ 10%
Buffer pool aliasing test 0 aliased addresses / 1000 acquire-release cycles
test_pipeline_M4 deadlock test completes within 5s timeout
moe_sched_buffer_pool_wait_us p99 (steady state) < 100 µs
moe_sched_overlap_fraction (M=2, balanced) ≥ 0.4
CUDA feature build (CUDA available) cargo build --features cuda clean

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/sched/m1_baseline.bin no overlap, reference output locked output tensor
fixtures/sched/m2_overlap.bin double-buffer, must match M=1 byte-identical to m1_baseline
fixtures/sched/m4_pipeline.bin 4 micro-batches correctness byte-identical to m1_baseline
fixtures/sched/buf_alias_check.bin two simultaneous handles no shared address bytes
fixtures/sched/deadlock_stress.bin M=8, alternating slow/fast batches all complete within 10s
fixtures/sched/replay_baseline_v1.bin locked scheduler output byte-identical across refactors
fixtures/sched/cuda_event_sync.bin event record/wait ordering GEMM output after a2a recv, no stale reads
fixtures/sched/m0_error.bin num_micro_batches=0 in config MoeError::InvalidConfig at construction

8.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Debug/Fix
Buffer released before combine reads it a2a for i+2 overwrites buffer used by combine of i BufferHandle Arc-ref-counted; pool releases slot only when all handles dropped
Deadlock from circular buffer dependency all micro-batches waiting for a buffer, none releasing pool_size=M·2, assert!(pool_size>num_micro_batches) at construction
CUDA event ordering inversion compute reads stale data, rare NaN in GEMM submit record+wait atomically under Mutex<StreamSyncState>
Uneven micro-batch split (n_tokens%M≠0) wrong total on concatenation pad or handle variable-size explicitly, strip padding before return
Work queue unbounded growth (slow GEMM consumer) moe_sched_work_queue_depth grows monotonically bounded back-pressure channel before injecting, cap M·2
Prefetch lookahead starvation main thread blocks in PrefetchHandle::wait() stream_count ≥ M+prefetch_depth
Buffer pool "resize" under memory pressure old handles point to freed memory pool fixed-size at construction; acquire() returns Err when full, never reallocs

8.7 Performance Concerns

Concern Impact Mitigation
Buffer pool spin wait µs/acquire 10µs spin then park_thread, avoid spin under high contention
Work queue steal overhead steals/s Injector amortizes; keep work items coarse (micro-batch, not token)
Thread-per-stage context switch µs/switch pin comm thread near NIC NUMA node, compute thread near GPU NUMA node
Micro-batch split allocation malloc/batch pre-allocated split-view Tensor::Slice, no copy
CUDA event overhead µs/event reuse pooled event objects (<2µs cudaEventRecord)
Deep pipeline (M>4) memory pressure GB pool size=M·2·buffer_size; cap M at mem_budget/(2·buffer_size)
Overlap measurement overhead % sample overlap fraction every 100 micro-batches, not per-batch

8.8 Edge Cases

  • num_micro_batches=1: no overlap, degenerates to serial, must match Phase-5 output exactly. n_tokens<M: some micro-batches empty (0 tokens), handled gracefully (no GEMM submitted, combine no-op).
  • Expert GEMM faster than a2a: overlap fraction near 0, correct but no speedup (warn if <0.05). a2a faster than expert: prefetch buffer idle longer, no correctness issue.
  • CUDA stream pool exhaustion: acquire() blocks up to 100ms then MoeError::StreamTimeout. Scheduler shutdown mid-pipeline: drain in-flight micro-batches via drain_and_join(timeout), partial forward = error.
  • config.buffer_depth<2: Scheduler::new returns MoeError::InvalidConfig unless num_micro_batches==1. Micro-batch reordering: micro_batch_id used as sort key in combine stage to reassemble in-order.

8.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Buffer aliasing output checksum mismatch, Miri UAF single forward pass corrupt Arc ref-count prevents premature release; fix pool size
Deadlock in pool acquire buffer_pool_wait_us > 1s all micro-batches stall timeout park_thread(10s) → PoolDeadlock; abort, drain, reset pool
CUDA event inversion intermittent NaN in GEMM output one micro-batch corrupt submit-lock already in place; re-run forward
Worker thread panic catch_unwind in worker one WorkItem lost MoeError::WorkerPanic; drain queue, restart scheduler
Stream timeout stream_timeout_total one forward pass fails MoeError::StreamTimeout; increase pool or reduce M

Postmortem: moe_sched_deadlocks_total, moe_sched_worker_panics_total, moe_sched_stream_timeouts_total, moe_sched_overlap_fraction (histogram).

8.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: moe_sched_overlap_fraction{pg_id}, moe_sched_buffer_pool_wait_us (histogram), moe_sched_work_queue_depth, moe_sched_micro_batch_latency_us{stage}, moe_sched_deadlocks_total, moe_sched_stream_timeouts_total.
  • Tracing: sched.micro_batch{id,stage} timestamps; sched.overlap_window (actual overlap duration); sched.buffer_acquire (wait time).
  • Logging: DEBUG per-stage entry/exit; INFO scheduler init (M, buffer_depth, stream_count, transport); WARN overlap_fraction<0.05 for >100 batches; ERROR deadlock/worker panic.
  • Tuning/deployment: num_micro_batches rule-of-thumb M=ceil(a2a_latency/gemm_latency) (--auto-tune-M); scheduler is per-process, stateless across forward calls (buffers recycled); CUDA streams/events allocated at new, freed on Drop, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES controls GPU assignment.
  • Rollback/compatibility: reducing M to 1 restores serial path safely; increasing M validated against mem_budget_gb first; SchedConfig version-stamped in checkpoint metadata; buffer_depth change doesn't affect weights (safe at inference time); --dump-micro-batch-timeline writes Gantt-chart JSON (Perfetto-compatible).

21. Phase 9 — Advanced routing — expert-choice routing, noisy top-k, hash/random routing, shared experts, routing-variant plug points

9.1 Objectives

  • Implement four routing variants beyond baseline dense top-k: expert-choice, noisy top-k, hash/random, shared-expert; expose RouterStrategy trait-object plug point in moe-gate, swappable without recompiling consumers.
  • Expert-choice: experts select tokens (capacity-bounded), eliminating token-drop but requiring a synchronous gather of scores across all tokens before dispatch. Noisy top-k: per-token i.i.d. Gaussian noise added to logits before top-k (noise_std_scale: f32 tunable).
  • Hash routing: deterministic token_idx % num_experts (or learned-hash), zero gate computation — ablation baseline. Shared expert: one or more always-active experts receive every token unconditionally, output summed with sparse MoE output.
  • Constraints: all variants satisfy token-conservation; emit RoutingDecision compatible with existing DispatchPlan pipeline; AuxLossState optional per variant.

9.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P1 (Shazeer noisy top-k), P4 (Zhou 2022 expert-choice), P9 (DeepSpeed-MoE shared expert), I1 (token conservation, all variants), I3 (routing determinism — noise seeded per forward), ADR-21 (feature flags for variant selection)

moe-gate/src/strategy.rs

pub trait RouterStrategy: Send + Sync {
    fn route(&self, logits: &GateLogits, cfg: &RouterConfig, rng: &mut dyn RngCore) -> Result<(Vec<RoutingDecision>, Option<AuxLossState>), MoeError>;
    fn variant_name(&self) -> &'static str;
}
pub enum RouterStrategyKind { TopK, NoisyTopK, ExpertChoice, Hash, Random, SharedExpert }

RouterConfig gains noise_std_scale, expert_choice_capacity, shared_expert_ids: Vec<ExpertId>, hash_fn; StrategyRegistry: HashMap<&'static str, Arc<dyn RouterStrategy>> loaded at runtime from moe-runtime config.

Noisy top-k (noisy_topk.rs) — N(0, softplus(W_noise·x)²) per Shazeer 2017; SmallRng seeded from per-layer routing_seed, advanced deterministically per forward; noise_std_scale=0 regresses exactly to baseline top-k. (P1, I3)

Expert-choice (expert_choice.rs, Zhou 2022) — experts rank tokens by gate score, each selects top-cap where cap=capacity_factor·n_tokens/num_experts; transpose score matrix [E×T], topk along token dim per expert, resolve >1-expert conflicts via token-level top-k on assigned experts; unselected tokens dropped (residual passthrough); full [n_tokens×num_experts] score matrix required before any dispatch (sync barrier, latency in §9.7). (P4)

Hash routing (hash_routing.rs) — expert_id = token_idx % num_experts (or cyclic (token_idx/stride)%num_experts for load balance), weight=1.0, no softmax; AuxLossState=None (no gradient through hash).

Shared expert (shared_expert.rs) — shared_expert_ids always weight=1.0 regardless of router output; emitted first in RoutingDecision, moe-dispatch skips capacity check for shared entries; weights stored in separate replicated SharedExpertWeights (no EP sharding); output = sparse_output + Σ shared_expert_outputs. (P9)

Wiring (moe-gate/src/lib.rs) — Gate::new(strategy: Box<dyn RouterStrategy>); TOML routing_strategy = "expert_choice"|"noisy_topk"|"hash"|"random"|"shared_expert"; moe-runtime reads LayerConfig, constructs via factory. (ADR-21)

Telemetry — routing_variant label on all routing metrics; expert_choice_conflict_rate counter; shared_expert_load gauge; span gate::route[strategy=<name>].

Tests — test_noisy_topk_noise_zero_equals_topk, test_expert_choice_no_over_capacity (count≤cap), test_hash_routing_deterministic, test_shared_expert_always_assigned, test_strategy_swap_runtime (mid-reload, dispatch unchanged).

9.3 Deliverable

  • moe-gate compiles with all five strategies behind RouterStrategy; moe-runtime selects from TOML, forward succeeds for each variant; moe-cli --routing-strategy expert_choice prints per-expert token counts.
  • Telemetry dashboards show routing_variant label + expert_choice_conflict_rate; moe-bench differential test emits routing_strategy_diff.json (each variant vs dense reference).

9.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • All moe-gate/tests/ pass; cargo test -p moe-gate green.
  • Noisy top-k noise_std_scale=0: bit-identical RoutingDecision to baseline top-k on same input.
  • Expert-choice: per-expert count ≤ ceil(capacity_factor·n_tokens/num_experts) over 10,000 random inputs (fuzz).
  • Hash routing: token_idx % num_experts verified for all token_idx ∈ [0,65535], no token dropped.
  • Shared expert: shared ids present in every RoutingDecision.topk, output added to final output.
  • Strategy-swap: zero-recompile switch produces valid forward output; noisy top-k adds < 5% latency vs baseline top-k at batch=512.

9.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture file Purpose Expected output
fixtures/routing/noisy_topk_seed42.json deterministic noisy decisions, seed=42 exact RoutingDecision JSON, stable round-trip
fixtures/routing/expert_choice_cap2.json n_tokens=8, E=4, cap=2 per-expert count=2, no double-assignment
fixtures/routing/hash_routing_16t_4e.json 16 tokens, 4 experts token i -> expert i%4, all weights=1.0
fixtures/routing/shared_expert_e0.json expert 0 always shared expert 0 in every RoutingDecision row
fixtures/routing/expert_choice_conflict.json forced multi-expert conflict resolved by token-level top-k, conflict_rate>0 emitted
fixtures/routing/strategy_switch.json config reload mid-run 2nd batch uses new strategy, 1st unchanged
fixtures/routing/noise_zero_regression.json noise_std_scale=0 bit-identical to baseline top-k fixture

Malformed: noise_std_scale<0 → MoeError::InvalidConfig; out-of-range shared_expert_ids → MoeError::InvalidExpertId.

9.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Fix
Expert-choice memory spike full [T×E] score matrix resident (2MB f32 at T=8192,E=64) pin allocation in arena, not stack
Conflict resolution divergence tie-break by ExpertId alone → non-reproducible enforce (score DESC, ExpertId ASC) sort
Noise seed drift under micro-batching determinism breaks across micro-batches advance seed by n_micro_tokens, not fixed stride
Shared expert + EP sharding EP routes tokens away from shared expert's rank shared expert executes locally on every rank before a2a
Hash routing uneven n_tokens%num_experts last expert gets fewer tokens capacity buffer sized for peak, never average
Strategy warm-swap race partial dispatch mixes old/new decisions guard StrategyRegistry with RwLock, read-lock per forward
AuxLoss None mishandled panic on backward for hash/random variant check Option<AuxLossState> before backward

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • Expert-choice O(E×T) sort: E independent topk calls (Rayon-parallel); at E=64,T=2048 ≈1.8M ops, acceptable if vectorized. Noise generation: n_tokens·num_experts floats/forward via SmallRng (~1.2 GB/s); negligible vs GEMM but cache-polluting, generate in-place into logits buffer.
  • Shared expert GEMM: runs on every rank if pre-a2a — schedule on separate CUDA stream to overlap with a2a (hook into moe-sched double-buffer). Strategy dispatch: single vtable call/forward, negligible vs GEMM.
  • Conflict resolution sort: O(k_avg·n_tokens), k_avg≈2-3, in-place on pre-allocated scratch. Tail latency: expert-choice's full-score-matrix barrier is absent in top-k — measure p99 gate latency separately.

9.8 Edge Cases

  • n_tokens=0: all strategies return empty Vec<RoutingDecision>, no panic. num_experts=1: trivial routing everywhere; shared expert id=0 dedup-guarded against double assignment.
  • k>num_experts: clamp to num_experts, warn (fixture k_overflow.json). capacity_factor=0.0 (expert-choice): zero cap, all tokens dropped, drop_rate=1.0.
  • shared_expert_ids overlapping routed experts: appears once in output (deduped in combine step). Noise NaN: clamp logits to [-50,50] before injection (test logits=f32::MAX).
  • Strategy returns empty topk: valid (token dropped), downstream handles as capacity-exceeded.

9.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Recovery
Expert-choice OOM allocator returns Err single forward pass MoeError::AllocationFailed; reduce batch, retry
Conflict resolution infinite loop iteration counter > E·T single layer assertion + error return; fix sort logic
Strategy swap mid-batch token count mismatch post-combine corrupted output RwLock prevents; drain in-flight batches before swap
Shared expert weight missing lookup returns None all tokens corrupt error at load time; reload + validate at startup
Noisy top-k seed collision across layers identical decisions in two layers reduced model quality per-layer seed namespace (layer_idx XOR seed)

Postmortem: routing_variant + expert_choice_conflict_rate + shared_expert_load in incident dashboard.

9.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: gate_routing_variant{variant} label on routing_entropy/expert_utilization/drop_rate; expert_choice_conflict_rate, shared_expert_tokens_per_batch, noise_std_scale gauges.
  • Tracing: gate::route[strategy,noise_std] span; child spans per expert for expert-choice sort.
  • Config reload: strategy swap drains in-flight forwards via Arc<RwLock<RouterStrategy>>, no restart; rollback = revert TOML, no weight reload.
  • Compatibility: DispatchPlan format unchanged across variants; telemetry schema additive (backward-compatible dashboards); deployment gate runs routing_strategy_diff.json before promotion, max divergence documented per variant.

22. Phase 10 — Quantization and memory management — int8/fp8 expert weights, weight offload/paging, activation memory pools

10.1 Objectives

  • Quantize expert FFN weights to int8 and fp8 (E4M3/E5M2) in moe-expert; dequantize per-GEMM or use quantized GEMM backend.
  • Implement weight offload/paging: expert weights resident on CPU DRAM, paged to GPU/accelerator on demand; LRU eviction policy.
  • Activation memory pool in moe-core: pre-allocated arena per forward pass; eliminates per-token allocations during dispatch/combine.
  • Constraints: quantization must not break token-conservation invariant; paging must be transparent to moe-dispatch/moe-expert callers; memory pool must be thread-safe for concurrent expert GEMMs.

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P15 (zero-copy weight access — ADR-09 safetensors mmap), I7 (weight shard completeness), ADR-04 (cuBLAS cublasGemmEx for int8), ADR-05 (Arena — ActivationArena reset pattern), P3 (bounded resource usage — max_resident in pager)

Quantized weight storage (moe-expert/src/quant.rs)

  • QuantKind { Int8, Fp8E4M3, Fp8E5M2, F16, BF16, F32 } added to ExpertWeights. (ADR-04)
  • QuantizedMatrix { data: Vec<u8>, scale: Vec<f32>, zero_point: Vec<f32>, shape: [usize;2], quant: QuantKind }.
  • Quantization: per-channel (row-wise for w_in, col-wise for w_out) using scale = max(|W|) / 127.0 for int8; absmax per-block for fp8.
  • Dequant path: dequant_f32(q: &QuantizedMatrix) -> Tensor; invoked before GEMM if backend does not support quantized GEMM.
  • Quantized GEMM path: FFI to cuBLAS cublasGemmEx (int8) or Transformer Engine fp8_gemm; scale tensors passed as separate buffers.
  • moe-expert/src/expert.rs: fn compute(weights: &ExpertWeightHandle, tokens: &Tensor, cfg: &ExpertConfig) -> Result<Tensor, MoeError> dispatches to quant or fp32 path based on ExpertConfig.quant_kind.

Weight paging (moe-expert/src/paging.rs)

  • WeightPageManager { lru: LruCache<ExpertId, DeviceBuffer>, max_resident: usize, page_size: usize }.
  • DeviceBuffer: GPU memory handle (opaque pointer via FFI); free on eviction.
  • WeightPageManager::ensure_resident(id: ExpertId) -> &DeviceBuffer: checks LRU; if miss, initiates async H2D transfer (pinned host memory → device); blocks until transfer complete before returning.
  • Async prefetch: moe-sched calls prefetch(expert_ids: &[ExpertId]) one micro-batch ahead; transfers overlap with GEMM of current batch (hook into double-buffer pipeline from Phase 7).
  • Pinned host memory pool: PinnedPool { slots: Vec<PinnedSlot> }; PinnedSlot reused across forward passes; avoids cudaMallocHost per page.
  • Eviction: LRU; never evict an expert currently executing (reference count guard: Arc<DeviceBuffer>); eviction logs weight_eviction_count metric.

Activation memory pool (moe-core/src/arena.rs)

  • ActivationArena { buf: Vec<u8>, cursor: AtomicUsize, capacity: usize }.
  • Arena::alloc(nbytes: usize, align: usize) -> *mut u8: bump allocator; returns MoeError::ArenaExhausted if over capacity.
  • Arena::reset(): cursor → 0; called at start of each forward pass; no individual frees.
  • Thread safety: AtomicUsize::fetch_add for cursor; concurrent allocs safe; reset requires exclusive access (caller holds &mut Arena).
  • Tensor handle gains arena_offset: Option<u32>; arena-backed tensors have no Box ownership; freed en-masse at reset.
  • ArenaConfig { capacity_bytes: usize, alignment: usize }; sized at startup based on n_tokens * d_model * 4 * 4 (four fp32 intermediates per token).
  • moe-dispatch, moe-expert, moe-gate accept arena: &mut ActivationArena parameter for temporary allocations.

Int8 calibration (moe-expert/src/calibration.rs) — calibrate(weights, kind) -> QuantizedMatrix: single-pass scale computation (tested via moe-bench); load_quantized(path, kind) -> Result<ExpertWeights, MoeError>: reads pre-quantized .q8/.fp8 files, validates header magic.

Tests — test_int8_quant_roundtrip (max rel error <1%), test_fp8_quant_roundtrip (<2%), test_arena_concurrent_alloc (16 threads, no overlap/panic), test_arena_reset_reuse (alloc→reset→alloc same offsets), test_paging_lru_eviction (resident ≤ max_resident after 100 accesses), test_prefetch_overlap (H2D completes before ensure_resident called).

10.3 Deliverable

  • moe-expert compiles with int8/fp8 quantized GEMM paths; FFI stubs for cuBLAS/TE integrated.
  • WeightPageManager manages up to max_resident expert weights on device; eviction/prefetch operational.
  • ActivationArena replaces per-call Vec allocations in moe-dispatch and moe-expert.
  • moe-cli --quant int8 --max-resident-experts 16 runs full forward pass; reports peak device memory.
  • moe-bench reports memory savings vs fp32 baseline and throughput delta.

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Int8 GEMM output vs fp32 reference: max per-element relative error < 1.5% on 1000 random batches.
  • Fp8 GEMM output vs fp32 reference: max per-element relative error < 3% on 1000 random batches.
  • Arena allocation: zero heap allocations inside forward() hot path (verified via jemalloc stats or MALLOC_CONF=stats_print:true).
  • Paging: device memory usage <= max_resident * expert_weight_bytes + 10% overhead at steady state.
  • Prefetch: ensure_resident wait time < 200 µs when prefetch called 1 batch ahead (measured via tracing span).
  • cargo test -p moe-expert + cargo test -p moe-core pass; no memory leaks (Valgrind clean on CPU-only path).

10.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected output
fixtures/quant/int8_weights_expert0.q8 Pre-quantized int8 expert 0 weights GEMM output within 1.5% of fp32 reference
fixtures/quant/fp8_weights_expert0.fp8 Pre-quantized fp8 expert 0 weights GEMM output within 3% of fp32 reference
fixtures/quant/calibration_reference.json Known-good scale/zero_point values calibrate() reproduces exactly
fixtures/arena/alloc_sequence.json Series of alloc/reset calls Offsets match expected bump sequence
fixtures/paging/lru_trace.json Sequence of expert accesses with evictions eviction_count and resident_set match expected
fixtures/quant/corrupt_header.q8 Malformed magic bytes MoeError::CorruptWeightFile
fixtures/quant/overflow_scale.json scale=0 edge case MoeError::InvalidQuantScale

10.6 Pitfalls

  • Dequant before GEMM doubles memory: fp32 dequantized copy + original int8 coexist; use arena for dequant buffer, release after GEMM; never hold both long-term.
  • Scale per-channel vs per-tensor: per-tensor scale is faster to apply but causes 3-5x higher quantization error on outlier-heavy weight matrices; always use per-channel.
  • Async H2D in wrong CUDA stream: prefetch must use a dedicated copy stream, not the compute stream; mixing streams causes stall or corruption; assign copy_stream separately in WeightPageManager.
  • Arena reset() while GEMM in flight: if reset() called before all expert GEMMs complete, buffers silently reused → data corruption; gate reset with barrier() ensuring all streams idle.
  • LRU eviction of active expert: reference count guard prevents eviction, but if caller forgets Arc clone, eviction proceeds; use ExpertGuard RAII wrapper that holds Arc<DeviceBuffer> for duration of GEMM.
  • fp8 underflow on small activations: E4M3 has min normal ≈ 1.95e-3; small activations flush to zero; detect via fp8_underflow_count metric; mitigate with per-block scaling.
  • Arena fragmentation on variable-length token batches: bump allocator has no fragmentation, but arena must be sized for worst-case batch; under-sizing causes ArenaExhausted mid-batch; size at 2×expected_max.

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • Int8 GEMM vs fp32: cuBLAS int8 GEMM ~2× throughput, ~4× memory bandwidth reduction; measure actual speedup per expert shape; may be regression for small experts (overhead of scale application dominates).
  • Pinned memory bandwidth: H2D bandwidth from pinned DRAM ≈ 12-16 GB/s PCIe 4; expert weight page = d_ff * d_model * 2 * 1 byte (int8) ≈ 2-4 MB; transfer ~200-400 µs; must overlap with GEMM to hide.
  • Arena alloc hot path: AtomicUsize::fetch_add is 2-4 ns; negligible; but reset() is O(1) (single atomic store) — far cheaper than per-allocation free.
  • LRU cache lock contention: LruCache protected by Mutex; concurrent expert GEMMs serialize on eviction check; use sharded LRU (shard by ExpertId % SHARDS) for high EP parallelism.
  • Fp8 E4M3 conversion overhead: software fp8 conversion on CPU ≈ 50 ns/element; at d_ff*d_model=8M elements that's 400 ms — unacceptable; must use hardware fp8 (H100+) or pre-convert at load time.
  • Cold start paging: first forward pass must page in all max_resident experts; warmup latency can be seconds; expose --warmup-rounds CLI flag that pre-pages without emitting output.

10.8 Edge Cases

  • max_resident=0: all experts paged out; every access triggers H2D; valid but pathologically slow; log warning at startup.
  • Expert weight file not found at paging time: ensure_resident returns MoeError::WeightNotFound; forward pass aborts cleanly.
  • Arena capacity = 0: alloc(0) returns null pointer (valid); alloc(1) returns ArenaExhausted; test both.
  • Quantized weight loaded on CPU-only build: int8 GEMM path falls back to dequant+fp32 GEMM; no panic; warn in log.
  • Weight page evicted between prefetch and use: ensure_resident re-pages; single retry; if device OOM on retry, return error.
  • n_tokens varies between batches: arena sized for max; smaller batches waste space but never exhaust; reset each pass.
  • Concurrent reset() calls: &mut Arena guarantees exclusive access at type level; no additional guard needed.

10.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Arena exhausted mid-pass ArenaExhausted error Single forward pass aborted Return error; drain batch Increase arena_capacity_bytes in config
H2D OOM on page-in CUDA OOM signal Single expert GEMM Evict more experts; retry Reduce max_resident; restart
Int8 scale=0 (zero matrix) InvalidQuantScale at calibration Expert outputs zero Guard at calibration; skip bad weight Recalibrate or use fp32 fallback
Concurrent arena reset+alloc Data corruption (rare race) Batch output corrupted Type system prevents (requires &mut) Fix calling code; review arena borrow
LRU deadlock on eviction Thread hangs in Mutex::lock All expert GEMMs stall Sharded LRU; lock timeout (500 ms) + abort Restart; investigate lock holder

Postmortem signals: weight_eviction_count, arena_exhausted_count, fp8_underflow_count, h2d_wait_us in incident dashboard.

10.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: weight_resident_count (gauge), weight_eviction_count (counter), h2d_transfer_us (histogram), arena_utilization_fraction (gauge), fp8_underflow_count (counter per expert).
  • Tracing: paging::ensure_resident[expert=N] span; arena::alloc[bytes=N] counted but not individually traced (too hot); quant::dequant[expert=N,kind=int8] span.
  • Config knobs: quant_kind, max_resident_experts, arena_capacity_bytes, prefetch_depth (how many batches ahead to prefetch); all runtime-reloadable except arena_capacity_bytes (requires restart).
  • Deployment: pre-quantize weights offline with moe-cli quant --kind int8 --output model.q8; runtime loads .q8 files; no on-the-fly quantization in production.
  • Rollback: revert quant_kind = "f32" in config; reload weights from fp32 files; no restart if using hot-reload path.
  • Migration: fp32 → int8 migration requires re-calibration pass; document calibration_dataset_size >= 512 batches for stable scales.
  • Compatibility: QuantizedMatrix serialization format versioned with header magic 0x4D4F45518 + version byte; old readers reject new format with clear error.

23. Phase 11 — Fault tolerance and determinism — rank-failure handling, expert-replica fallback, deterministic replay from routing seed

11.1 Objectives

  • Detect and recover from EP rank failures without aborting the entire forward pass where possible.
  • Implement expert-replica fallback: if the rank owning expert e fails, a replica rank serves it; replica routing transparent to moe-dispatch.
  • Guarantee deterministic replay: given identical inputs + routing_seed, produce bit-identical RoutingDecision, DispatchPlan, and outputs regardless of restart, re-execution, or micro-batch split.
  • Constraints: fault tolerance must not introduce correctness regressions on the happy path; determinism guarantee must hold across all routing strategies (Phase 9); replay mechanism must integrate with moe-telemetry for audit.

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: I1 (token conservation — dropped tokens on rank failure take residual passthrough), I3 (routing determinism — RoutingSeed guarantees replay), I4 (EP ownership — primary rank = expert_id % world_size), I10 (A2A symmetric counts — must be rebuilt on rank exclusion), ADR-06 (tokio HeartbeatMonitor task), P6 (fail fast — MoeError::RankFailed)

Rank failure detection (moe-comm/src/health.rs)

  • HeartbeatMonitor { interval: Duration, timeout: Duration, peer_status: HashMap<u32, Instant> }. (ADR-06 tokio task)
  • Background task (Tokio task or OS thread) sends PingMsg to each peer rank every interval; updates peer_status on PongMsg.
  • is_alive(rank: u32) -> bool: returns false if now - last_pong > timeout.
  • RankFailureEvent { rank: u32, detected_at: Instant } emitted to moe-runtime via mpsc channel.
  • On detection: moe-comm marks rank as Dead; ongoing all-to-all returns MoeError::RankFailed(rank) with partial results.

Expert replica management (moe-expert/src/replica.rs)

  • ReplicaMap { primary: HashMap<ExpertId, EpRank>, replicas: HashMap<ExpertId, Vec<EpRank>> }.
  • Populated at startup from ClusterConfig.replica_factor: u32 (default 1 = no replicas, 2 = one backup per expert).
  • Expert e primary on rank = e % world_size; replica on (e + world_size/2) % world_size for factor=2.
  • moe-runtime queries ReplicaMap when RankFailureEvent received; reroutes dispatch to replica rank.
  • DispatchPlan updated: tokens destined for failed rank redirected; all-to-all skips failed rank; replica rank handles tokens for both its own experts and replicated experts.

Failover all-to-all (moe-comm/src/a2a_failover.rs)

  • On RankFailed(r): rebuild send_counts and recv_counts excluding rank r; substitute replica rank r' in address map.
  • failover_a2a(plan: &DispatchPlan, failed_ranks: &[EpRank]) -> Result<A2AResult, MoeError>: variant of a2a that skips failed ranks and adjusts counts symmetrically.
  • If no replica available for expert e on failed rank: drop tokens for e; count in drop_rate_due_to_failure metric; residual passthrough for dropped tokens.

Deterministic replay (moe-core/src/replay.rs)

  • RoutingSeed { layer_idx: u32, forward_pass_id: u64, micro_batch_idx: u32 } → u64 seed via hash(layer_idx, forward_pass_id, micro_batch_idx).
  • ReplayLog { seed: RoutingSeed, routing_decision: Vec<RoutingDecision>, dispatch_plan: DispatchPlan } serialized to disk in bincode format.
  • ReplayWriter in moe-telemetry: opt-in via Config.replay_log_path; writes one entry per forward pass.
  • ReplayReader::verify(inputs: &Tensor, log: &ReplayLog) -> Result<bool, MoeError>: re-executes routing from same seed; asserts bit-identical decisions.
  • Determinism invariants: SmallRng seeded from RoutingSeed; no HashMap iteration in routing hot path (use BTreeMap or sorted Vec); tie-breaking by ExpertId ASC (locked in spec).

Replay-based failure investigation (moe-cli/src/replay.rs)

  • moe-cli replay --log forward_pass_42.bin --inputs batch42.bin: replays and prints per-token routing decisions; diffs against logged decisions; highlights divergences.

Tests — test_rank_failure_detection (is_alive false within 2·interval+timeout), test_replica_failover (rank 0 killed, replica serves expert 0), test_no_replica_drop (no replica → tokens dropped, drop_rate_due_to_failure>0), test_deterministic_replay (10 passes same seed → identical RoutingDecision), test_replay_log_roundtrip (write→read→verify true), test_partial_a2a_failure (one rank dead, remaining tokens correctly dispatched/combined).

11.3 Deliverable

  • moe-comm detects rank failure within 2×heartbeat interval; emits RankFailureEvent.
  • moe-runtime routes around failed ranks using replica map; forward pass completes (possibly with higher drop rate).
  • Replay log written per forward pass when configured; moe-cli replay reproduces routing decisions bit-identically.
  • moe-telemetry reports rank_failure_count, failover_latency_ms, drop_rate_due_to_failure.
  • moe-bench fault-tolerance scenario: inject rank failure mid-run; measure throughput degradation and recovery time.

11.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • HeartbeatMonitor detects failure within 2 * interval + timeout; test with interval=50ms, timeout=200ms → detection ≤ 300ms.
  • Forward pass with one dead rank (replica available): output matches reference (no silent corruption); MoeError not returned.
  • Forward pass with one dead rank (no replica): drop_rate_due_to_failure proportional to num_experts / world_size; no panic.
  • Deterministic replay: 1000 forward passes with same (inputs, seed) → bit-identical RoutingDecision every time; verified by ReplayReader::verify.
  • Replay log read/write roundtrip: 0 bit divergences on 100 randomly generated passes.
  • cargo test -p moe-comm -p moe-core pass; fault injection tests pass with mock transport.

11.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected output
fixtures/fault/rank_fail_ep4.json EP=4, rank 1 fails, replica available Output matches no-failure reference; rank_failure_count=1
fixtures/fault/rank_fail_no_replica.json EP=4, rank 1 fails, no replica drop_rate_due_to_failure ≈ 0.25; no panic
fixtures/fault/replay_seed42_pass0.bin Replay log for seed=42, pass=0 ReplayReader::verify returns true
fixtures/fault/replay_diverge.bin Replay log with tampered RoutingDecision verify returns false; diff highlights row
fixtures/fault/a2a_partial_fail.json All-to-all with ranks 0,3 dead Correct dispatch for surviving experts
fixtures/fault/heartbeat_timeout.json Mock heartbeat timeout sequence is_alive(rank=2) false after 300ms
fixtures/fault/corrupt_replay_log.bin Truncated bincode MoeError::CorruptReplayLog

11.6 Pitfalls

  • False-positive rank failure on GC pause / OS jitter: heartbeat timeout set too tight (< 500ms) triggers failover on healthy rank experiencing GC-like stall; set timeout >= 500ms; add warn log before failover.
  • Replica weight staleness: if replica weights diverge from primary (e.g., after online weight update), outputs differ silently; enforce weight sync on any weight update before serving; version tag in ReplicaMap.
  • Determinism broken by HashMap iteration order: HashMap<ExpertId, _> in DispatchPlan construction; iteration order non-deterministic across Rust versions; replace with BTreeMap or sort output before emitting.
  • Replay log disk I/O blocking forward pass: synchronous write to replay log adds latency; use async write via background Tokio task; bound channel depth to prevent unbounded memory.
  • forward_pass_id collision under restart: if counter resets to 0 on restart, logs from different runs collide; use (start_time_ns, counter) as composite key.
  • Failover doubles expert load on replica rank: replica rank now handles 2× expert count; its GEMM queue saturates; cap replica factor at 1 for large EP groups or increase replica rank capacity.
  • Partial all-to-all with asymmetric counts: after removing failed rank, send/recv count arrays must be recomputed symmetrically; off-by-one in count adjustment → deadlock in collective.

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • Heartbeat overhead: world_size - 1 pings per interval; at EP=64, 63 pings/interval; use batched ping (one broadcast, all reply); O(world_size) messages not O(world_size^2).
  • Failover latency: detection + replica lookup + rebuilt all-to-all ≈ 2×interval + replan_time; replan_time < 1ms (BTreeMap lookup + Vec rebuild); total ≈ 300-600ms for typical settings — acceptable for non-interactive inference.
  • Replay log write throughput: one RoutingDecision per token per layer; at T=4096, 8 layers, 1000 passes = 33M records; bincode encoding ≈ 100 MB/min; use ring buffer + periodic flush.
  • Replica synchronization on weight update: blocking sync doubles weight-update time; only sync on explicit sync_replicas() call, not on every update; document consistency window.
  • BTreeMap vs HashMap in routing hot path: BTreeMap O(log E) vs HashMap O(1); at E=64 difference is 6 comparisons vs 1 hash; acceptable; cache-friendly for small E.
  • Concurrent failover + new forward pass: moe-runtime processes RankFailureEvent between forward passes; if event arrives mid-pass, queued for next pass; no mid-pass plan mutation.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • All ranks fail simultaneously: failover_a2a returns MoeError::NoRanksAvailable; forward pass aborted; process exits cleanly.
  • Rank recovers after failover: HeartbeatMonitor detects recovery (3 consecutive pongs); marks rank Alive; ReplicaMap reverts to primary; log rank_recovery_count.
  • routing_seed all-zeros: valid; SmallRng initialized with zero seed produces deterministic (non-trivial) sequence; test explicitly.
  • Replay log for pass not found: ReplayReader returns MoeError::ReplayNotFound; moe-cli replay prints clear error.
  • Expert replica on same physical host as primary: failure of host kills both; ReplicaMap should warn if primary and replica share host; cross-host placement validation at startup.
  • micro_batch_idx overflow (> u32::MAX): seed hash wraps; document as invariant; at 60 fps * 1 year = 1.9B < u32::MAX — safe for inference; warn if exceeded.
  • Zero-replica configuration with EP=1: single-rank; rank failure = total failure; no fallback; moe-cli warns at startup.

11.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Rank fails, no replica HeartbeatMonitor Tokens for that expert dropped Residual passthrough; drop_rate_due_to_failure alert Add replica or reduce EP group
Replica weight out-of-sync Output divergence vs reference Corrupted layer output Version tag mismatch → error at sync Re-sync replica weights; reload
Replay log corruption CorruptReplayLog on read Audit capability lost Log error; continue inference Delete corrupt log; re-enable logging
Failover deadlock in a2a All EP ranks hang Entire forward pass Timeout on collective (5s) + abort Kill and restart process group
Heartbeat flood on large EP Network saturation Latency spike for all ranks Batched broadcast ping Reduce heartbeat frequency or batch
False failover on straggler Healthy rank evicted Higher drop rate Increase timeout; add jitter Mark rank live on pong; re-add to group

Postmortem: rank_failure_count, failover_latency_ms, drop_rate_due_to_failure, rank_recovery_count all in incident dashboard; replay log enables offline root-cause analysis.

11.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: rank_failure_count (counter), failover_latency_ms (histogram), drop_rate_due_to_failure (gauge per layer), rank_recovery_count (counter), replica_sync_lag_ms (gauge).
  • Tracing: comm::heartbeat[rank=N] span (sampled 1%); runtime::failover[failed_rank=N,replica=M] span on each failover event; replay::write[pass_id=N] span.
  • Replay log: Config.replay_log_path = "/var/log/moe/replay/" enables logging; replay_log_max_gb caps disk usage; oldest logs rotated first.
  • Alert thresholds: rank_failure_count > 0 → page on-call; drop_rate_due_to_failure > 0.05 → critical alert; failover_latency_ms p99 > 1000 → investigate heartbeat settings.
  • Deployment: replica factor set at cluster config time; changing requires restart (ReplicaMap rebuilt); expert weight sync runs on startup for each replica.
  • Rollback: disable fault-tolerance via Config.fault_tolerance = false; reverts to abort-on-rank-failure behavior; no data migration.
  • Compatibility: ReplayLog bincode format versioned; old logs readable by new reader (additive fields); new logs rejected by old reader with clear version error.
  • Runbook: rank failure → check rank_failure_count metric → inspect failover_latency_ms → verify drop_rate_due_to_failure < threshold → if replica unavailable, escalate for weight redistribution.

24. Phase 12 — Observability and profiling — per-expert utilization, routing entropy, drop rate, comm-vs-compute time, flamegraph workflow

12.1 Objectives

Instrument every pipeline stage with zero-hot-path-allocation telemetry; surface metrics/traces/profiles without core code changes.

Goal Constraint
Per-expert utilization (tokens/capacity per step) atomic counters, no Mutex on critical path
Routing entropy H=-Σp·log(p) over experts computed post-gate, before dispatch
Drop rate (global + per-expert) derived from DispatchPlan.dropped BitSet
Comm-vs-compute time ratio per forward pass wall-clock via moe-sched stream timestamps
Flamegraph workflow (pprof+inferno) works with cargo flamegraph, no patching
Token imbalance CV, aux-loss magnitude, z-loss magnitude gauges; CV alert if > 0.5; tapped from moe-gate

Constraints: moe-telemetry is the only crate calling metrics::*/tracing::*; all others emit via thin #[inline(always)] probes it exports (no dependency inversion); --features no-telemetry elides all probes to ().

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P4 (observability by default), ADR-11 (tracing crate), ADR-12 (metrics+prometheus), ADR-13 (OTLP tracing), I1 (token conservation — dropped_tokens_total), I8 (aux loss — moe_aux_loss gauge)

moe-telemetry/src/lib.rs

pub fn record_routing(decision: &RoutingDecision, n_tokens: usize, layer_id: u32);
pub fn record_dispatch(plan: &DispatchPlan, layer_id: u32);
pub fn record_expert_compute_ns(expert_id: ExpertId, layer_id: u32, elapsed_ns: u64);
pub fn record_a2a_ns(direction: A2aDir, layer_id: u32, elapsed_ns: u64);
pub fn record_combine(buffer: &CombineBuffer, layer_id: u32);
pub fn record_aux_loss(state: &AuxLossState, layer_id: u32);

moe-telemetry/src/metrics.rs — metrics crate + metrics-exporter-prometheus (pull, port 9100); counters moe_tokens_total{layer,expert}/moe_dropped_tokens_total{layer,expert}; gauges moe_expert_utilization, moe_routing_entropy, moe_drop_rate, moe_comm_compute_ratio, moe_aux_loss, moe_z_loss (all {layer} or {layer,expert}); histograms moe_expert_compute_ns{layer,expert} (1µs–100ms buckets), moe_a2a_ns{layer,dir}.
